## 6. 后端接口设计

### 6.1 接口总览

| 编号 | 方法 | 路径 | 说明 | 权限 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **验配单主档** | | | | |
| API-01 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings` | 验配单分页列表 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-02 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}` | 验配单详情（含从表） | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-03 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings` | 新建验配单（支持 `Idempotency-Key`） | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-04 | PUT | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}` | 更新验配单基础信息 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-05 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/void` | 作废验配单（仅草稿） | `m10:orthok:delete` |
| API-06 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/dashboard` | 工作台首页统计 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| **准入评估** | | | | |
| API-07 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies` | 申请列表 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-08 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies` | 提交准入评估 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-09 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies/{apply_no}` | 评估详情 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-10 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies/{apply_no}/review` | 医学复核 | `m10:orthok:audit` |
| API-11 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies/{apply_no}/consent` | 上传知情同意扫描件 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| **角膜地形图参数** | | | | |
| API-12 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/cornea` | 角膜参数列表（按眼别） | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-13 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/cornea` | 保存角膜参数 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-14 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/cornea/{cornea_id}/lock` | 锁定角膜参数记录 | `m10:orthok:audit` |
| API-15 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/cornea/{cornea_id}/invalidate` | 作废角膜参数记录 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| **试戴片台账与占用** | | | | |
| API-16 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses` | 试戴片台账列表 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-17 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses` | 新增试戴片 | `m10:orthok:trial` |
| API-18 | PATCH | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses/{id}/status` | 试戴片状态变更（消毒/报废） | `m10:orthok:trial` |
| API-19 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses/match` | 试戴片智能匹配 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-20 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/occupy` | 占用试戴片（互斥） | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-21 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/{id}/release` | 释放试戴片 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-22 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/active` | 当前占用清单 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-23 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/{id}/evaluation` | 保存试戴评估与订片决策 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| **定制参数与下单** | | | | |
| API-24 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/custom-params` | 定制参数列表 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-25 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/custom-params` | 生成定制参数 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-26 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{id}/fit-check` | 调 M01-08 适配校验 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-27 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{id}/order` | 确认下单下发厂家 | `m10:orthok:audit` |
| **到片验收与发放** | | | | |
| API-28 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/deliveries/pending` | 待到片验收/待发放队列 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-29 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/deliveries/accept` | 逐片验收 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-30 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/deliveries/{id}/disinfect` | 消毒登记 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-31 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/deliveries/issue` | 发放镜片 | `m10:orthok:sign` |
| **随访与并发症** | | | | |
| API-32 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/due` | 随访到期清单 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-33 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups` | 保存随访记录 | `m10:orthok:create` |
| API-34 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/followups` | 随访历史 | `m10:orthok:view` |
| API-35 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/{id}/report` | 不良事件上报 | `m10:orthok:report` |
| **停戴与换片** | | | | |
| API-36 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/stop` | 登记停戴 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-37 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/resume` | 复戴 | `m10:orthok:approve` |
| API-38 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/replace` | 启动换片 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| API-39 | POST | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/close` | 完结验配单 | `m10:orthok:update` |
| **统计与导出** | | | | |
| API-40 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/statistics/fitting` | 验配量与通过率统计 | `m10:orthok:stat` |
| API-41 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/statistics/followup` | 随访依从率与脱管统计 | `m10:orthok:stat` |
| API-42 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/statistics/complication` | 并发症与工作量统计 | `m10:orthok:stat` |
| API-43 | GET | `/api/v1/m10/ortho-k/export` | 导出 Excel | `m10:orthok:export` |
| **内部契约** | | | | |
| API-44 | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/{fit_no}` | **P-09 取 OK 镜验配单与定制参数** | 内部服务 |
| API-45 | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/by-patient/{patient_id}/active` | 患者当前在戴镜验配单 | 内部服务 |
| API-46 | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{fit_no}` | 取定制参数（供 M10-06 开单） | 内部服务 |
| API-47 | GET | `/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/due` | 复查到期清单（供 M10-09） | 内部服务 |
| API-48 | POST | `/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/on-sales-order` | M16-04 销售单回写 | 内部服务 |

> **接口总数：48 个**（对外 43 个 + 内部契约 5 个）。
> **P-09 契约核对：** API-44 路径 `GET /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/{fit_no}` 与共享规范 §8 P-09 **一字不差**。

### 6.2 统一响应格式与错误码

**统一响应格式**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {},
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**失败示例**

```json
{
  "code": "1004019",
  "message": "该试戴片已被他人占用（张×× 于 09:42 占用），请重新选择",
  "data": null,
  "trace_id": "b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9"
}
```

**分页格式**

```json
{
  "items": [],
  "total": 0,
  "page": 1,
  "page_size": 20,
  "pages": 0
}
```

**本子模块错误码表（`"1004001"`~`"1004040"`）**

| 错误码 | 含义 | HTTP | 触发场景 | 用户提示文案 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `0` | 成功 | 200 | — | — |
| `40001` | 参数校验失败 | 400 | 通用入参错误 | 请检查输入内容 |
| `40300` | 无权限 | 403 | 缺权限编码 | 您没有该操作权限 |
| `40400` | 资源不存在 | 404 | 记录不存在 | 数据不存在或已删除 |
| `40900` | 版本冲突（乐观锁） | 409 | `version` 不匹配 | 数据已被他人修改，请刷新后重试 |
| `42200` | 业务规则校验失败 | 422 | 通用业务规则 | 业务校验未通过 |
| `50000` | 服务器内部错误 | 500 | 未捕获异常 | 系统繁忙，请稍后重试 |
| `"109001"` | M10 无此权限 | 403 | 模块级无权限 | 您没有 M10 视光业务的操作权限 |
| `"1004001"` | 验配单不存在或已作废 | 404 | `fit_no` 查无或 `status = 'CLOSED'` 且作废 | 验配单不存在或已作废 |
| `"1004002"` | 准入评估未完成，不能进入下一环节 | 422 | 评估 `PENDING` 时试戴/定制 | 请先完成准入评估 |
| `"1004003"` | 验配单非草稿状态，不可修改或作废 | 422 | 非 `DRAFT` 调 API-05/PUT 全量 | 验配单已提交，不可修改 |
| `"1004004"` | 当前状态不允许该操作 | 422 | 状态机前置状态不符 | 当前状态不允许该操作 |
| `"1004005"` | 年龄不符合验配要求 | 422 | 年龄 <8 或 >40 | 年龄 7 岁，未满 OK 镜最低验配年龄 8 岁 |
| `"1004006"` | 近视度数超出可验配范围 | 422 | sph 超 −0.75~−6.00 | 球镜 −6.50D 超出 −0.75D~−6.00D 范围 |
| `"1004007"` | 散光超出可验配范围 | 422 | cyl 超 −1.50（配置） | 散光 −1.75D 超出 −1.50D 上限 |
| `"1004008"` | 屈光参差超出允许范围 | 422 | 双眼差 >1.50D | 屈光参差 2.00D 超出 1.50D 上限 |
| `"1004009"` | 角膜曲率超出可验配范围 | 422 | Km 超 41.00~46.00 | Km 46.50D 超出 41.00~46.00D 范围 |
| `"1004010"` | 角膜厚度不足 | 422 | CCT <480μm | 中央角膜厚度 470μm 小于 480μm |
| `"1004011"` | 眼压超出允许范围 | 422 | IOP 超 10~21mmHg | 眼压 22.5mmHg 超出 10~21mmHg 范围 |
| `"1004012"` | 泪液检查不达标 | 422 | BUT<10s 或 Schirmer<10mm | BUT 8.0s 小于 10s |
| `"1004013"` | 角膜地形图异常，禁止验配 | 422 | 圆锥可疑/不规则 | 地形图判定为圆锥角膜可疑，禁止验配 |
| `"1004014"` | 睑裂或眼表条件不适合 | 422 | 睑裂闭合不全/重度炎症 | 睑裂闭合不全，不适合夜间佩戴 |
| `"1004015"` | 依从性评估不达标 | 422 | 评分 <60 | 依从性评分 55 分，低于 60 分 |
| `"1004016"` | 未签署知情同意书 | 422 | `parent_consent_signed = FALSE` | 未上传知情同意书，无法通过评估 |
| `"1004017"` | 准入结论已失效，需重新评估 | 422 | 评估后 >90 天 | 准入评估已超 90 天有效期，请重新评估 |
| `"1004018"` | 角膜参数记录不可用 | 422 | 已锁定/已作废/非 PRE_FIT | 该角膜参数记录已锁定，不可修改 |
| `"1004019"` | 试戴片已被占用 | 409 | 部分唯一索引冲突 | 该试戴片已被他人占用（张×× 09:42），请重新选择 |
| `"1004020"` | 试戴片不可用 | 422 | 过期/损坏/已报废 | 试戴片已于 2026-10-01 过期，不可使用 |
| `"1004021"` | 医疗器械注册证无效 | 422 | 注册证为空/过期 | 该产品注册证已过期（2026-09-30），不可下单 |
| `"1004022"` | 试戴片消毒超期 | 422 | 距上次消毒 >7 天 | 该片已 8 天未消毒，请先消毒 |
| `"1004023"` | 试戴评估结论不允许生成定制参数 | 422 | 决策为 `RETRY`/`ABORT` | 该试戴决策为换片再试，不可用于生成参数 |
| `"1004024"` | 未完成适配校验 | 422 | 未调 `fit-check` 即下单 | 必须先调用适配校验才能下单 |
| `"1004025"` | 参数与推荐值偏差过大需二次确认 | 422 | `PASS_WITH_WARN` 未确认 | 参数与推荐值偏差较大，请确认后下单 |
| `"1004026"` | 适配校验未通过 | 422 | `fit = false` 或校验服务不可用 | 适配校验未通过：角膜曲率 47.20D 超出范围 |
| `"1004027"` | OK 镜产品不可用 | 422 | 产品停用/删除 | 产品已停用，请联系 M01 维护 |
| `"1004028"` | 弧参数或直径不符合规范 | 422 | `RC<BC<AC<PC` 不满足 | 弧参数关系错误：必须满足 RC < BC < AC < PC |
| `"1004029"` | 到片验收不通过 | 422 | 参数不一致/外观异常 | 参数存在不一致项（BC），不可验收通过 |
| `"1004030"` | 发放条件不满足 | 422 | 未消毒/教学<30 分钟/未签字 | 首次戴镜教学必须满 30 分钟（当前 25 分钟） |
| `"1004031"` | 镜片编号无效或重复 | 409 | `lens_no` 冲突/未绑定 | 镜片编号 OK-30-0281 已绑定患者王××，请核对 |
| `"1004032"` | 验配师资质不符合要求 | 403 | 资质缺失/失效 | 该员工不具备 OK 镜验配资质 |
| `"1004033"` | 随访记录校验失败 | 422 | 节点重复/处置意见缺失/严重并发症未上报 | D7 节点已有随访记录，不可重复录入 |
| `"1004034"` | 停戴登记不完整 | 422 | 原因 <10 字/感染未上报 | 停戴原因说明必须不少于 10 字 |
| `"1004035"` | 换片条件不满足 | 422 | 已换片/原因与复用方式冲突 | 屈光度变化必须重新验配，不可复用原参数 |
| `"1004036"` | 复戴条件不满足 | 422 | 停戴 >7 天未重新评估 | 停戴超过 7 天，复戴前必须重新做配适评估 |
| `"1004037"` | 验配单存在未结事项，不可完结 | 422 | 有未完成随访/待发放 | 存在未完成的随访或待发放镜片，不可完结 |
| `"1004038"` | 试戴片实物编号已存在 | 409 | `trial_lens_no` 冲突 | 试戴片编号 TL-0795-325-001 已存在 |
| `"1004039"` | 内部契约调用参数或状态不符 | 422 | 内部接口入参缺失 | 内部契约调用参数不合法 |
| `"1004040"` | 上游服务不可用 | 503 | M01-08/M03/M10-09 不可用 | 上游服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试 |

> **错误码使用约束：** 全部落在 `"1004001"`~`"1004040"` 号段内；`"109001"` 为模块通用码（照抄共享规范 §11）。

### 6.3 接口详细设计

#### API-01 验配单分页列表

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings
    ?page=1&page_size=20&keyword=张&status=FOLLOWING
    &store_id=101&fitter_id=2001&start_date=2026-10-01&end_date=2026-10-31
    &followup_due_only=false&overdue_only=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 10241,
        "fit_no": "OK202610050002",
        "patient_name": "张××",
        "age": 11,
        "gender": "M",
        "product_kind": "ORTHO_K",
        "product_name": "梦戴维 OK镜 标准型",
        "status": "FOLLOWING",
        "replace_round": 1,
        "fitter_name": "李×",
        "delivered_at": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+08:00",
        "expected_replace_date": "2027-10-31",
        "next_followup_date": "2026-11-07",
        "overdue_days": 0,
        "has_severe_complication": false
      }
    ],
    "total": 128,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 7
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 解析分页与筛选，`page_size` 上限 100 2) 按数据权限过滤 `store_id`（非主管仅本门店）3) `keyword` 匹配姓名（`idx_m10_ortho_k_fitting_name_trgm`）/单号/手机号 4) `followup_due_only=true` 追加 `next_followup_date <= CURRENT_DATE` 且状态在戴镜集合，走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_fitting_next_fu` 5) `overdue_only=true` 追加 `overdue_days > 0`，走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_fitting_overdue` 6) 默认排序 `created_at DESC`，`overdue_only` 时按 `overdue_days DESC` 7) 返回 `FittingListOut`。

**错误码：** `40100`、`"109001"`

#### API-02 验配单详情

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 10241,
    "fit_no": "OK202610050002",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "张××",
    "age": 11,
    "product_kind": "ORTHO_K",
    "product_code": "OPOK000012",
    "device_reg_no": "国械注进20203160001",
    "device_reg_valid_until": "2028-05-31",
    "fitter_name": "李×",
    "status": "FOLLOWING",
    "replace_round": 1,
    "delivered_at": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+08:00",
    "expected_replace_date": "2027-10-31",
    "next_followup_date": "2026-11-07",
    "overdue_days": 0,
    "followup_count": 1,
    "apply": {"id": 8812, "access_result": "PASS", "age": 11},
    "cornea_records": [],
    "trial_uses": [],
    "custom_params": [],
    "deliveries": [],
    "followups": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `fit_no` 查主档 2) 并行加载从表 `apply`（1:1）、`cornea_records`、`trial_uses`、`custom_params`、`deliveries`、`followups`（各 ≤50 条，超出分页）3) 组装 `FittingDetailOut` 4) 身份证脱敏（前 6 后 4）。

**错误码：** `"1004001"`

#### API-03 新建验配单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings
Idempotency-Key: 8a2b1c3d-4e5f-6789-abcd-ef0123456789
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "product_kind": "ORTHO_K",
  "product_code": "OPOK000012",
  "store_id": 101,
  "fitter_id": 2001,
  "assistant_id": 2015,
  "optometry_no": "YG202610310001",
  "od_sphere": -3.25, "od_cylinder": -0.75, "od_axis": 180,
  "os_sphere": -3.50, "os_cylinder": -0.50, "os_axis": 175,
  "previous_fit_id": null,
  "remark": "初次验配"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"id": 10241, "fit_no": "OK202610310005", "status": "DRAFT", "apply_id": null},
  "trace_id": "c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等（Redis `m10:idem:ortho_k:create:{key}` TTL 24h）2) 调 M03-01 校验患者并取姓名/生日算年龄 3) 调 M01-02 校验验配师资质 `qualification ∈ {OPTOMETRIST, OPHTHALMIC_PHYSICIAN}`，否则 `"1004032"` 4) 调 M01-08 `full-config` 取产品、注册证、供货周期；注册证无效 `"1004021"`，产品停用 `"1004027"` 5) 校验柱镜-轴位配对与 0.25 步进 `"1004001"` 6) 患者 <18 岁校验监护人信息 7) `previous_fit_id` 非空时校验原单存在且 `replace_round+1` 8) 生成 `fit_no`（Redis `INCR m10:code:OK:{yyyyMMdd}`）9) 写主表 `status='DRAFT'` 10) 写日志 `CREATE_FITTING`。

**错误码：** `"1004032"`、`"1004021"`、`"1004027"`、`"1004001"`

#### API-04 更新验配单基础信息

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}
Content-Type: application/json

{"remark": "家长要求下周再试戴", "assistant_id": 2016, "version": 2}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"fit_no": "OK202610310005", "version": 3},
  "trace_id": "d4e5f6a7b8c9d0e1"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `fit_no` + `version` 乐观锁更新 2) 仅允许改 `remark`/`assistant_id`/`phone`/`guardian_*`/`review_doctor_id`；屈光度与产品字段在非 `DRAFT` 状态不可改 `"1004003"` 3) 版本冲突 `40900` 4) 写日志并记录 `changed_fields`。

**错误码：** `"1004003"`、`40900`

#### API-05 作废验配单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/void
Content-Type: application/json

{"void_reason": "患者家长决定暂不验配，退回定金", "version": 3}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"fit_no": "OK202610310005", "status": "CLOSED"},
  "trace_id": "e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `status='DRAFT'` 可作废，否则 `"1004003"` 2) `void_reason` ≥10 字，否则 `40001` 3) 置 `status='CLOSED'`、`void_reason`、`voided_by`、`voided_at`、`closed_at` 4) 释放所有未释放的试戴占用 5) 写日志 `VOID_FITTING` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1004003"`、`40001`

#### API-08 提交准入评估

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/applies
Idempotency-Key: 1a2b3c4d-5e6f-7890-abcd-ef0123456789
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "age": 11,
  "compliance_score": 85,
  "compliance_note": "家长配合度高，已完成问卷",
  "parent_consent_signed": true,
  "consent_file_id": 66012,
  "consent_signer_name": "张父",
  "consent_signer_relation": "FATHER",
  "od_sphere": -3.25, "od_cylinder": -0.75, "od_axis": 180,
  "os_sphere": -3.50, "os_cylinder": -0.50, "os_axis": 175,
  "od_k1": 42.75, "od_k2": 43.50, "os_k1": 42.80, "os_k2": 43.20,
  "od_cornea_thickness": 528, "os_cornea_thickness": 525,
  "od_iop": 15.5, "os_iop": 16.0,
  "tear_break_up_time": 12.0, "schirmer_test": 15.0,
  "topography_pattern": "NORMAL",
  "keratoconus_suspect": false,
  "eyelid_condition": "NORMAL",
  "conjunctiva_condition": "NORMAL",
  "meibomian_gland": "NORMAL"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 8812,
    "apply_no": "OKA202610310003",
    "access_result": "PASS",
    "fail_items": [],
    "age_check_passed": true,
    "sphere_check_passed": true,
    "cylinder_check_passed": true,
    "curvature_check_passed": true,
    "thickness_check_passed": true,
    "iop_check_passed": true,
    "tear_check_passed": true,
    "topography_check_passed": true,
    "eyelid_check_passed": true,
    "anisometropia": 0.25,
    "anisometropia_check_passed": true,
    "doctor_review_required": false
  },
  "trace_id": "b8c9d0e1f2a3b4c5"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 按 §3.3.1 逐项判定 12 项指标并写各 `*_check_passed` 3) 计算 `anisometropia = abs(od_sphere − os_sphere)` 4) 汇总判定：全 TRUE → `PASS`；1~2 项 FALSE 且均属可放宽项（age/thickness 475~479/tear/anisometropia）→ `CONDITIONAL` 且 `doctor_review_required=TRUE`；其他 → `FAIL` 并把全部不达标项写入 `fail_items` 5) 地形图为 `ASYMMETRIC_BOWTIE` 时强制 `doctor_review_required=TRUE` 6) PASS/CONDITIONAL 时校验知情同意与扫描件，否则 `"1004016"` 7) 生成 `apply_no` 8) PASS/CONDITIONAL 时自动创建关联验配单 9) 写日志 `SUBMIT_APPLY`/`ASSESS_APPLY` 10) 返回全部判定结果。

**错误码：** `"1004005"`~`"1004016"` 逐项、`"1004013"`、`40001`

#### API-13 保存角膜参数

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/cornea
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "measure_stage": "PRE_FIT",
  "measure_source": "MANUAL",
  "k1": 42.75,
  "k2": 43.50,
  "axis_k2": 92,
  "e_value": 0.48,
  "hvid": 12.00,
  "pupil_light": 3.20,
  "pupil_dark": 5.80,
  "thickness_central": 528,
  "thickness_thinnest": 521,
  "topography_pattern": "NORMAL",
  "keratoconus_suspect": false,
  "topography_report_id": 77012,
  "device_code": "PENTACAM_001"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 9921,
    "cornea_no": "OCT202610310008",
    "km": 43.13,
    "cornea_astigmatism": 0.75,
    "k1_mm": 7.89,
    "k2_mm": 7.76,
    "recommended_bc": 7.95,
    "recommended_diameter": 11.00
  },
  "trace_id": "a3b4c5d6e7f8a9b0"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验验配单状态 ∈ {APPLYING, ASSESSED, TRIAL, CUSTOMIZING, FOLLOWING} 2) 服务端重算 `km=(k1+k2)/2`、`cornea_astigmatism=k2−k1`、`k1_mm=337.5/k1`、`k2_mm=337.5/k2`（不采信前端值）3) 校验 `k1≤k2`、最薄点≤中央、暗瞳≥明瞳 4) 图形为圆锥可疑/不规则或 `keratoconus_suspect=TRUE` 时 `topography_report_id` 必填，否则 `"1004018"` 5) 生成 `cornea_no` 6) 按 §3.4.1/§3.4.2 计算并返回 `recommended_bc`/`recommended_diameter`（仅提示，不落库）7) 写表 8) 发布 `m10.orthok.cornea.saved` 9) 写日志 `SAVE_CORNEA`。

**错误码：** `40001`、`"1004018"`、`"1004004"`

```
PATCH /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses/{id}/status
Content-Type: application/json

{

  "disinfect_method": "BOIL",

```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",

    "id": 501,
    "status": "IN_STOCK",
    "disinfect_count": 46,
    "last_disinfected_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "f8a9b0c1d2e3f4a5"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 状态流转校验（DISINFECTING→IN_STOCK、任意→DAMAGED、任意→SCRAPPED）2) DISINFECTING→IN_STOCK 时必须传消毒方式与时间，`disinfect_count+1` 3) →SCRAPPED 时 `scrap_reason` ≥10 字 4) →DAMAGED 时 `damage_note` ≥5 字 5) 存在未释放占用时不可变更 `"1004019"` 6) 写日志。

**错误码：** `"1004019"`、`40001`

#### API-19 试戴片智能匹配

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-lenses/match
    ?fit_no=OK202610310005&eye_side=OD&bc_tolerance=0.10&reduction_tolerance=0.25
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "basis": {
      "k1": 42.75,
      "kf_mm": 7.89,
      "target_reduction": -3.25,
      "recommended_bc": 7.95,
      "recommended_diameter": 11.00,
      "delta_mm": 0.05,
      "formula": "BC = 337.5 / 42.75 + 0.05 = 7.95mm"
    },
    "items": [
      {
        "id": 501,
        "trial_lens_no": "TL-0795-325-001",
        "bc": 7.95,
        "reduction_power": -3.25,
        "k_min": 40.41,
        "k_max": 41.91,
        "k_in_range": true,
        "bc_diff": 0.00,
        "reduction_diff": 0.00,
        "status": "IN_STOCK",
        "usage_count": 38,
        "score": 100
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a9b0c1d2e3f4a5b6"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 取该眼 PRE_FIT 角膜记录，无则 `"1004018"` 2) 按 §3.4.1 计算 `kf_mm=337.5/k1`、`recommended_bc=ROUND(kf_mm+delta,2)` 对齐 0.05 步进 3) 按 §3.4.2 计算 `recommended_diameter` 对齐 0.10 步进并限幅 10.00~11.00 4) 查询在库、未过期、消毒未超期的试戴片，BC 在 `recommended_bc±bc_tolerance`、降度在 `target±reduction_tolerance` 5) `score = 100 − bc_diff×100 − reduction_diff×20`，使用次数少者优先 6) 按 `k_in_range DESC, score DESC` 排序，最多 20 条。

**错误码：** `"1004018"`、`"1004028"`

```json
    "trial_lens_no": "TL-0795-325-001",
    "eye_side": "OD",
    "trial_seq": 1,
    "occupied_at": "2026-10-31T10:08:00+08:00",
    "released_at": null,
    "occupied_by_name": "李×"
  },
  "trace_id": "b0c1d2e3f4a5b6c7"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `Idempotency-Key` 幂等 2) 验配单状态 ∈ {ASSESSED, TRIAL}，否则 `"1004004"` 3) 准入有效期 ≤90 天，否则 `"1004017"` 4) 试戴片 `status='IN_STOCK'`（否则 `"1004020"`）、未过期（`"1004020"`）、消毒未超 7 天（`"1004022"`）5) **直接 INSERT `m10_ortho_k_trial_use`（`released_at=NULL`），由部分唯一索引 `uk_m10_ortho_k_trial_use_active` 实现互斥**；捕获 `UniqueViolationError` 且约束名匹配时返回 `"1004019"` 并附当前占用者 6) 成功后台账 `status='IN_USE'` 7) 验配单 `status='TRIAL'`、首次 `trial_started_at` 落库 8) 生成 `use_no` 9) 写日志 `OCCUPY_TRIAL`。

**错误码：** `"1004019"`、`"1004020"`、`"1004022"`、`"1004017"`、`"1004004"`

#### API-21 释放试戴片

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/{id}/release
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "release_kind": "RETURNED",
  "disinfected_after": true,
  "disinfect_method": "BOIL",
  "disinfected_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:00+08:00",
  "damage_found": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 12007,
    "released_at": "2026-10-31T10:18:00+08:00",
    "release_kind": "RETURNED",
    "occupied_minutes": 10,
    "trial_lens_status": "IN_STOCK"
  },
  "trace_id": "c1d2e3f4a5b6c7d8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 记录存在且 `released_at IS NULL`，否则 `"1004004"` 2) 校验权限（当前用户占用 或 `m10:orthok:trial`）3) 写 `released_at`、`release_kind`、`released_by`、`occupied_minutes` 4) 已消毒则台账 `status='IN_STOCK'`、`last_disinfected_at`、`disinfect_count+1`；未消毒则 `status='DISINFECTING'` 5) `damage_found=TRUE` 时台账 `status='DAMAGED'` 6) `usage_count+1` 7) 写日志 `RELEASE_TRIAL` 8) 释放后该行不再被部分唯一索引覆盖，同一片可被下一位患者占用。

**错误码：** `"1004004"`、`40300`

**错误码：** `"109001"`

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/trial-uses/{id}/evaluation
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fitting_id": 10241,
  "fluo_central": "IDEAL",
  "fluo_reverse": "IDEAL",
  "fluo_align": "IDEAL",
  "fluo_peripheral": "IDEAL",
  "fit_quality": "EXCELLENT",
  "lens_position": "CENTERED",
  "lens_movement": 0.80,
  "coverage_ratio": 98.0,
  "va_trial": 0.8,
  "over_refraction_sph": -0.25,
  "tft_performed": true,
  "tft_pattern": "BULLSEYE",
  "tft_ring_diameter": 3.40,
  "tft_ring_integrity": "COMPLETE",
  "tft_report_id": 77015,
  "decision": "ADJUST",
  "decision_note": "配适优秀但追加球镜-0.25D，建议降度调整为-3.50D",
  "suggested_bc": 7.95,
  "suggested_reduction": -3.50,
  "suggested_diameter": 11.00
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 12007,
    "fit_quality": "EXCELLENT",
    "decision": "ADJUST",
    "fitting_status": "CUSTOMIZING",
    "suggested_bc": 7.95,
    "suggested_reduction": -3.50,
    "suggested_diameter": 11.00
  },
  "trace_id": "e3f4a5b6c7d8e9f0"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验试戴记录存在且未重复评估 `"1004004"` 2) `tft_performed=TRUE` 时 `tft_pattern` 与 `tft_report_id` 必填 3) 决策校验：`fit_quality='UNACCEPTABLE'` 时不得选 ORDER_AS_IS/ADJUST；`RETRY`/`ABORT` 时 `decision_note` ≥10 字；`ADJUST` 时三个 `suggested_*` 必填，均否则 `"1004023"` 4) 写评估字段 5) 状态推进：ORDER_AS_IS/ADJUST → `CUSTOMIZING`；RETRY → 保持 `TRIAL`；ABORT → `STOPPED` 6) 写日志 `SAVE_TRIAL_EVAL` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1004023"`、`"1004004"`、`40001`

#### API-25 生成定制参数

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/custom-params
Idempotency-Key: 3c4d5e6f-7890-abcd-ef01-234567890123
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "trial_use_id": 12007,
  "cornea_id": 9921,
  "param_source": "TRIAL",
  "bc": 7.95,
  "rc": 7.25,
  "ac": 8.25,
  "pc": 9.20,
  "diameter": 11.00,
  "target_reduction": -3.25,
  "actual_reduction": -3.50,
  "over_refraction": -0.25,
  "reduction_adjust_reason": "试戴片追加球镜-0.25D，并入实际降度为-3.50D",
  "material": "BOSTON XO",
  "color": "CLEAR",
  "manufacturer_code": "MDW",
  "manufacture_lead_days": 20
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 6601,
    "custom_no": "OKC202610310006",
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "check_result": "PENDING",
    "reduction_diff": -0.25,
    "price_ref": 3800.0000,
    "fitting_status": "CUSTOMIZING"
  },
  "trace_id": "a5b6c7d8e9f0a1b2"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等 2) 校验验配单 `status='CUSTOMIZING'`，否则 `"1004004"` 3) 校验所引用 `cornea_id`：存在、`is_valid=TRUE`、`measure_stage='PRE_FIT'`，否则 `"1004018"` 4) 校验 `trial_use_id`（如有）：决策 ∉ {RETRY, ABORT}，否则 `"1004023"` 5) 弧参数校验 `RC<BC<AC<PC` 且 `直径>BC`，否则 `"1004028"` 6) 服务端计算 `reduction_diff = actual − target`；差值非 0 时 `reduction_adjust_reason` ≥10 字，否则 `"1004028"` 7) 调 M01-08 `pricing` 取 `price_ref` 快照（失败不阻断，仅置空） 8) 同眼已有有效参数时更新而非新建（`uk_m10_ortho_k_custom_param_eye`）9) **锁定所引用的角膜记录** `is_locked=TRUE` 10) 写 `m10_ortho_k_custom_param`（`check_result='PENDING'`）11) 写日志 `SAVE_CUSTOM_PARAM`。

**错误码：** `"1004018"`、`"1004023"`、`"1004028"`、`"1004004"`

#### API-26 调 M01-08 适配校验（fit-check）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{id}/fit-check
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "product_code": "OPOK000012",
  "cornea_k": 43.13,
  "cornea_thickness": 528,
  "astigmatism": -0.75,
  "myopia": -3.25,
  "age": 11
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 6601,
    "check_result": "PASS_WITH_WARN",
    "fit_check_passed": true,
    "fit_check_at": "2026-10-31T10:15:32+08:00",
    "fit_check_message": "适配良好，建议使用参数组 8.20/-3.00",
    "fit_check_result": {
      "fit": true,
      "product_code": "OPOK000012",
      "checks": [
        {"item": "角膜曲率", "value": 43.13, "range": "41.00~46.00", "passed": true},
        {"item": "角膜厚度", "value": 528, "range": "≥480", "passed": true},
        {"item": "散光", "value": -0.75, "range": "≤-2.50", "passed": true},
        {"item": "近视度数", "value": -3.25, "range": "-6.00~0.00", "passed": true},
        {"item": "年龄", "value": 11, "range": "8~40", "passed": true}
      ],
      "recommended_params": [
        {"base_curve": 8.20, "reduction_power": -3.00, "diameter": 10.6}
      ],
      "followup_days": "1,7,30,90",
      "lead_time_days": 20
    },
    "deviations": [
      {"item": "BC", "actual": 7.95, "recommended": 8.20, "diff": -0.25, "warn": true},
      {"item": "降度", "actual": -3.50, "recommended": -3.00, "diff": -0.50, "warn": true}
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "b6c7d8e9f0a1b2c3"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 取定制参数与验配单，组装入参 `product_code/cornea_k/cornea_thickness/astigmatism/myopia/age`（`cornea_k` 取所引用角膜记录的 `km`）2) 调 M01-08 `GET /internal/v1/optical/ortho-k/fit-check`（API-38），超时 3s 3) **完整回执写入 `fit_check_result` JSONB**（不裁剪 `checks[]` 与 `recommended_params[]`），写 `fit_check_at`、`fit_check_passed`、`fit_check_message` 4) 判定 `check_result`：
   - `fit=false` 或任一 `checks[].passed=false` → `FAIL`，`check_fail_items` 存不通过项名称
   - `fit=true` 且本单 BC/降度/直径与 `recommended_params[0]` 偏差均 ≤0.50（mm 或 D）→ `PASS`
   - 偏差任一 >0.50 → `PASS_WITH_WARN`
5) 上游不可用/超时 → `"1004026"`，`check_result` 保持 `PENDING`（**绝不允许降级为通过**）6) 写日志 `FIT_CHECK`（含耗时 `duration_ms`）。

**错误码：** `"1004026"`（校验未通过或上游不可用）、`"1004027"`（产品不可用）、`"1004040"`（上游 503）

#### API-27 确认下单下发厂家

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{id}/order
Idempotency-Key: 4d5e6f78-90ab-cdef-0123-456789012345
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "confirm_warn": true,
  "confirm_note": "患者K1 42.75D略平于推荐参数组基准，采用7.95基弧更贴合，降度按试戴结果-3.50D更精准",
  "manufacturer_code": "MDW",
  "manufacture_lead_days": 20,
  "expected_arrival_date": "2026-11-20"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 6601,
    "custom_no": "OKC202610310006",
    "status": "ORDERED",
    "is_ordered": true,
    "ordered_at": "2026-10-31T10:20:00+08:00",
    "expected_arrival_date": "2026-11-20",
    "fitting_status": "ORDERED"
  },
  "trace_id": "c7d8e9f0a1b2c3d4"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等 2) 校验 `check_result <> 'PENDING'`，否则 `"1004024"` 3) 校验 `check_result <> 'FAIL'`，否则 `"1004026"` 4) `check_result='PASS_WITH_WARN'` 时必须 `confirm_warn=true` 且 `confirm_note` ≥10 字，否则 `"1004025"` 5) **再次校验注册证有效期** `device_reg_valid_until >= ordered_at::date`，否则 `"1004021"` 6) 调 M01-08 `full-config` 确认产品 `status='ENABLED'`，否则 `"1004027"` 7) 校验供货周期 1~90 8) 置 `status='ORDERED'`、`is_ordered=TRUE`、`ordered_at`、`ordered_by`、`expected_arrival_date` 9) 两眼都下单后验配单转 `ORDERED` 并写 `ordered_at` 10) 发布事件 `m10.orthok.fitting.created`（供 M10-06 开单）11) 写日志 `ORDER_CUSTOM` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1004024"`、`"1004025"`、`"1004026"`、`"1004021"`、`"1004027"`

**业务逻辑：** 1) 统计 5 个状态计数：PRODUCING（在产）、PENDING_ACCEPT（`accept_result='PENDING' AND arrived_at IS NOT NULL`，走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_delivery_pending`）、PENDING_ISSUE（`accept_result='PASS' AND delivered_at IS NULL`，走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_delivery_undelivered`）、overdue_arrival（`status='ORDERED' AND expected_arrival_date < CURRENT_DATE`）、RETURNED 2) `type=ACCEPT` 只返回待验收，`type=ISSUE` 只返回待发放，缺省全返回 3) 排序 `arrived_at ASC`（先到先验）。

#### API-29 逐片验收

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/deliveries/accept
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fitting_id": 10241,
  "custom_param_id": 6601,
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "lens_no": "OK-30-0281",
  "arrived_at": "2026-10-31T09:00:00+08:00",
  "package_intact": true,
  "actual_bc": 7.95,
  "actual_rc": 7.25,
  "actual_ac": 8.25,
  "actual_pc": 9.20,
  "actual_diameter": 11.00,
  "actual_reduction": -3.50,
  "actual_material": "BOSTON XO",
  "appearance_ok": true,
  "scratch_found": false,
  "chip_found": false,
  "bubble_found": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 8810,
    "delivery_no": "OKD202610310003",
    "accept_result": "PASS",
    "param_match": true,
    "mismatch_items": [],
    "fitting_status": "ARRIVED"
  },
  "trace_id": "e9f0a1b2c3d4e5f6"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `custom_param_id` 未被验收（`uk_m10_ortho_k_delivery_param`） 2) **逐项比对**定制参数与实物参数（BC/RC/AC/PC/直径/降度/材质/颜色，容差 0.001 对齐 2 位小数），任一不一致则 `param_match=FALSE` 且 `mismatch_items` 记录字段名 3) 外观检查：`appearance_ok=TRUE` 时三个缺陷标记必须为 FALSE 4) 结论：参数全一致 + 外观合格 → `PASS`；否则 `FAIL`（此时仍保存，供后续退换） 5) `lens_no` 全局唯一（`uk_m10_ortho_k_delivery_lens_no`），冲突 `"1004031"` 6) 参数不一致却传 `PASS` → `"1004029"` 7) 写 `m10_ortho_k_delivery`，`custom_param.status='ARRIVED'` 8) 两眼都 `PASS` 时验配单转 `ARRIVED` 9) 写日志 `ACCEPT_DELIVERY` / `REJECT_DELIVERY`。

**错误码：** `"1004029"`（验收不通过）、`"1004031"`（编号重复）、`"1004004"`（重复验收）

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验验发记录存在且 `accept_result='PASS'`，否则 `"1004029"` 2) `disinfect_method ∈ {BOIL, PEROXIDE, UV}`，否则 `40001` 3) 写 `disinfected=TRUE`、`disinfected_at`、`disinfect_method` 4) 写日志 `DISINFECT_DELIVERY`。

**错误码：** `"1004029"`、`40001`

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等 2) 逐条校验验发记录：`accept_result='PASS'`、`delivered_at IS NULL`、`disinfected=TRUE`，否则 `"1004030"` 3) **教学时长硬校验** `teaching_minutes >= 30`，否则 `"1004030"` 4) 校验 `teaching_content` 非空且 `teaching_signed=TRUE`、`receiver_sign_file_id` 非空，否则 `"1004030"` 5) 校验 `receiver_relation`：患者为未成年时不得为 `SELF` 6) `replace_months` ∈ 6~24 7) 写 `delivered_at`、`delivered_by`、`wear_start_date`、`replace_months`、`expected_replace_date = wear_start_date + replace_months 月`、教学信息 8) 验配单转 `DELIVERED`，写 `delivered_at`、`expected_replace_date` 9) 按 §3.4.6 计算 8 个复查节点，`next_followup_date = D1` 10) 调 M10-09 P-26 `POST /internal/v1/m10/followups/on-created` 生成复查计划；失败时置 `followup_plan_synced=false` 并放入补偿队列 `m10:orthok:sync:followup_plan` 11) 发布事件 `m10.orthok.fitting.created` 12) 写日志 `DELIVER_LENS` + `TEACHING`。

**错误码：** `"1004030"`（教学/消毒/签字不满足）、`"1004031"`（编号未绑定）、`"1004004"`（状态不符）、`40001`

#### API-32 随访到期清单

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/due
    ?store_id=101&due_within_days=0&overdue_only=false&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "fitting_id": 10241,
        "fit_no": "OK202610050002",
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "张××",
        "phone": "13800001234",
        "guardian_phone": "13900005678",
        "delivered_at": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+08:00",
        "next_followup_date": "2026-11-07",
        "next_followup_node": "D7",
        "overdue_days": 0,
        "completed_nodes": ["D1"],
        "missed_nodes": []
      }
    ],
    "total": 12,
    "node_summary": {"D1": 3, "D7": 2, "D30": 4, "D90": 3}
  },
  "trace_id": "b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 筛选 `status IN ('DELIVERED','FOLLOWING','REPLACE_DUE')` 且 `next_followup_date <= CURRENT_DATE + due_within_days`，走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_fitting_next_fu` 2) `overdue_only=true` 时追加 `overdue_days > 0` 3) 用 `jsonb_agg` 聚合已完成节点（附录 C.3 同款查询）4) 按 `next_followup_date ASC` 排序 5) 汇总 `node_summary` 各节点待查人数 6) 结果不缓存（实时性要求）。

**错误码：** `"109001"`

#### API-33 保存随访记录

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups
Idempotency-Key: 6f7890ab-cdef-0123-4567-890123456789
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fitting_id": 10241,
  "followup_node": "D7",
  "followup_type": "SCHEDULED",
  "scheduled_date": "2026-11-07",
  "visited_at": "2026-11-08T10:20:00+08:00",
  "subjective_complaints": "无不适，视力良好",
  "wearing_hours_per_night": 9.0,
  "wearing_days_per_week": 7,
  "compliance_check": "GOOD",
  "od_va_uncorrected": 1.0,
  "os_va_uncorrected": 1.0,
  "od_fluorescein": "NORMAL",
  "os_fluorescein": "NORMAL",
  "od_fluorescein_grade": 0,
  "os_fluorescein_grade": 0,
  "od_lens_position": "CENTERED",
  "os_lens_position": "CENTERED",
  "od_lens_cleanliness": "CLEAN",
  "os_lens_cleanliness": "CLEAN",
  "od_tft_pattern": "BULLSEYE",
  "os_tft_pattern": "BULLSEYE",
  "complication_grade": "NONE",
  "followup_result": "NORMAL",
  "followup_doctor_id": 3001,
  "operator_id": 2001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 15021,
    "followup_no": "OKF202611080003",
    "followup_node": "D7",
    "followup_day": 8,
    "visit_late_days": 1,
    "followup_count": 2,
    "fitting_status": "FOLLOWING",
    "next_followup_date": "2026-11-30",
    "next_followup_node": "D30",
    "plan_synced": true
  },
  "trace_id": "c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等 2) 校验验配单 `status IN ('DELIVERED','FOLLOWING','REPLACE_DUE','STOPPED')`，否则 `"1004004"` 3) 校验节点唯一（`uk_m10_ortho_k_followup_node`，`EXTRA` 除外），重复 `"1004033"` 4) 计算 `followup_day = visited_at::date − wear_start_date`；`visit_late_days = visited_at::date − scheduled_date` 5) 校验结论规则：非 `NORMAL` 时 `disposal_advice` ≥10 字；`DISCONTINUE` 时必须有并发症或角膜染色 STAINING/INFILTRATE/ULCER；`complication_grade='SEVERE'` 时 `report_required=TRUE` 且 `complications` 非空；均否则 `"1004033"` 6) 染色非正常时染色位置必填 7) 计算 `next_followup_node` 与 `next_followup_date`（`OBSERVE` 时可显式指定 2 周后 `EXTRA`）8) 写 `m10_ortho_k_followup` 9) 主表 `followup_count+1`、`last_followup_at`、`next_followup_date`；若首次随访且 `status='DELIVERED'` 则转 `FOLLOWING` 10) 调 M10-09 更新计划为 `COMPLETED`，失败置 `plan_synced=false` 入补偿队列 11) 写日志 `SAVE_FOLLOWUP`。

**错误码：** `"1004033"`、`"1004004"`、`40001`

#### API-35 不良事件上报

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/{id}/report
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "report_id": "AE202611080012",
  "reported_at": "2026-11-08T15:30:00+08:00",
  "recipient": "MEDICAL_AFFAIRS",
  "event_narrative": "患者D7复查发现右眼角膜中央2mm浸润灶伴前房反应，已立即停戴并转诊眼科急诊",
  "disposal_measures": "已转诊眼科急诊，给予左氧氟沙星滴眼液每小时一次，48小时后复诊观察",
  "stopped": true,
  "stopped_at": "2026-11-08"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 15021,
    "report_id": "AE202611080012",
    "reported_at": "2026-11-08T15:30:00+08:00",
    "fitting_status": "STOPPED",
    "has_severe_complication": true
  },
  "trace_id": "e5f6a7b8c9d0e1f2"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验随访记录存在且 `report_required=TRUE`，否则 `"1004004"` 2) 校验尚未上报（`report_id IS NULL`），否则 `"1004004"` 3) `event_narrative` ≥20 字、`disposal_measures` ≥20 字，否则 `40001` 4) 写 `report_id`、`reported_at` 5) 若 `complication_grade='SEVERE'`，主表 `has_severe_complication=TRUE` 6) `stopped=true` 时联动停戴：主表转 `STOPPED`，写 `stop_reason_code='INFECTION'`（或对应原因）、`stop_reason`、`stopped_at` 7) 调 M02-06 审计（`POST /internal/v1/m02/audit/record`）8) 写日志 `REPORT_COMPLICATION` + snapshot。

**错误码：** `"1004004"`（无需上报/已上报）、`40001`（字数不足）

#### API-36 登记停戴

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/stop
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "stop_reason_code": "CORNEAL_INJURY",
  "stop_reason": "D90复查发现右眼上方角膜点状染色2级，需停戴观察并加强护眼",
  "stopped_at": "2026-09-18T10:00:00+08:00",
  "expected_stop_days": 14,
  "handling": "OBSERVE"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fit_no": "OK202606200003",
    "status": "STOPPED",
    "stop_reason_code": "CORNEAL_INJURY",
    "expected_resume_date": "2026-10-02",
    "plans_cancelled": 4
  },
  "trace_id": "f6a7b8c9d0e1f2a3"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验验配单状态非终态（`REPLACED`/`CLOSED` 不可停戴），否则 `"1004004"` 2) `stop_reason` ≥10 字，否则 `"1004034"` 3) `stopped_at >= delivered_at`，否则 `40001` 4) 原因 ∈ {CORNEAL_INJURY, INFECTION} 时必须已上报或同时置 `report_required`，否则 `"1004034"` 5) 写 `status='STOPPED'`、`stop_reason_code`、`stop_reason`、`stopped_at` 6) 调 M10-09 把未执行的复查计划置 `CANCELLED` 7) 计算 `expected_resume_date = stopped_at + expected_stop_days` 8) 安排停戴期间护理提醒（D7/D30/D90 发 M03-05 提醒）9) 写日志 `STOP_WEAR`。

**错误码：** `"1004034"`、`"1004004"`、`40001`

#### API-37 复戴

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/resume
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "resumed_at": "2026-10-02T10:00:00+08:00",
  "cornea_id": 9935,
  "fit_confirmed": true,
  "remark": "停戴14天后复查角膜完全恢复，镜片配适良好，恢复戴镜"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fit_no": "OK202606200003",
    "status": "FOLLOWING",
    "resumed_at": "2026-10-02T10:00:00+08:00",
    "next_followup_date": "2026-12-31"
  },
  "trace_id": "a7b8c9d0e1f2a3b4"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验验配单 `status='STOPPED'`，否则 `"1004004"` 2) 计算停戴天数 `resumed_at − stopped_at` 3) **停戴 >7 天时强制要求重新配适评估**：必须传 `cornea_id`（一条 `measure_stage='POST_STOP'` 的记录）且 `fit_confirmed=TRUE`，否则 `"1004036"` 4) 停戴 ≤7 天可直接复戴 5) 清空 `stop_reason`/`stopped_at`（`ck_m10_ortho_k_fitting_stop_clean` 要求），`status='FOLLOWING'` 6) 重新排程后续复查节点并同步 M10-09 7) 写日志 `START_REPLACE`（复戴动作）。

**错误码：** `"1004036"`（停戴>7天未评估）、`"1004004"`、`40001`

#### API-38 启动换片

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/replace
Idempotency-Key: 7a8b9c0d-ef01-2345-6789-012345678901
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "replace_reason_code": "CYCLE_EXPIRED",
  "reuse_params": false,
  "remark": "戴满12个月，按周期换片"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "old_fit_no": "OK202510230012",
    "old_status": "REPLACED",
    "new_fit_no": "OK202610310021",
    "replace_round": 2,
    "previous_fit_id": 8112
  },
  "trace_id": "b8c9d0e1f2a3b4c5"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等 2) 校验原单 `status IN ('FOLLOWING','REPLACE_DUE')`，否则 `"1004004"` 3) 校验原单未被换过（无 `previous_fit_id = 原单 id` 的子单），否则 `"1004035"` 4) `replace_reason_code ∈ {POWER_CHANGE, POOR_FIT}` 时 `reuse_params` 必须为 FALSE，否则 `"1004035"` 5) 原单置 `status='REPLACED'`、`replace_reason_code` 6) 新建验配单：`replace_round = 原单+1`、`previous_fit_id = 原单 id`，复制患者/监护/屈光度初值，`status='DRAFT'` 7) **准入评估必须重新做**（不继承原单 `access_result`）8) 发布事件 `m10.orthok.fitting.created` 9) 写日志 `START_REPLACE`。

**错误码：** `"1004035"`、`"1004004"`

#### API-39 完结验配单

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/fittings/{fit_no}/close
Content-Type: application/json

{"close_reason": "换片完成，本副镜片到期，原验配单完结归档"}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {"fit_no": "OK202510230012", "status": "CLOSED", "closed_at": "2026-10-31T12:00:00+08:00"},
  "trace_id": "c9d0e1f2a3b4c5d6"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验状态 ∈ {`STOPPED`,`REPLACED`}，否则 `"1004004"` 2) 校验无未结事项：无未释放试戴占用、无 `accept_result='PASS' AND delivered_at IS NULL`、无 `report_required=TRUE AND report_id IS NULL`，否则 `"1004037"` 3) 置 `status='CLOSED'`、`closed_at` 4) 写日志 `CLOSE_FITTING`。

**错误码：** `"1004037"`（有未结事项）、`"1004004"`

```
GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/statistics/complication
    ?start_date=2026-01-01&end_date=2026-10-31&store_id=101
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "summary": {
      "total": 86,
      "moderate_plus": 19,

    },

    ],
```

**响应**

```json
{

    "task_id": "exp_20261031_0001",
    "export_type": "FITTING",
```
