```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `export_type ∈ {FITTING, FOLLOWUP, COMPLICATION, TRIAL_LENS, ALL}` 2) 创建异步导出任务，投递 Celery `m10.orthok.export` 3) 返回 `task_id`，前端轮询 `GET /api/v1/m10/ortho-k/export/{task_id}`（复用本接口的查询参数，返回 `download_url`） 4) 单次导出上限 50,000 行，超出提示缩小日期范围 5) 写日志 `EXPORT` 并调 M02-06 审计（导出含患者信息）。

**错误码：** `40001`（类型非法/超限）、`"109001"`

#### API-44 取 OK 镜验配单与定制参数（P-09 内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/{fit_no}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fit_no": "OK202610310005",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "张××",
    "age": 11,
    "product_kind": "ORTHO_K",
    "product_code": "OPOK000012",
    "product_name": "梦戴维 OK镜 标准型",
    "device_reg_no": "国械注进20203160001",
    "device_reg_valid_until": "2028-05-31",
    "fitter_id": 2001,
    "status": "ORDERED",
    "replace_round": 1,
    "od_sphere": -3.25,
    "os_sphere": -3.50,
    "od_target_reduction": -3.25,
    "os_target_reduction": -3.50,
    "custom_params": [
      {
        "custom_no": "OKC202610310006",
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "bc": 7.95,
        "rc": 7.25,
        "ac": 8.25,
        "pc": 9.20,
        "diameter": 11.00,
        "target_reduction": -3.25,
        "actual_reduction": -3.50,
        "material": "BOSTON XO",
        "color": "CLEAR",
        "check_result": "PASS_WITH_WARN",
        "is_ordered": true,
        "expected_arrival_date": "2026-11-20"
      },
      {
        "custom_no": "OKC202610310007",
        "eye_side": "OS",
        "bc": 7.95,
        "rc": 7.25,
        "ac": 8.25,
        "pc": 9.20,
        "diameter": 11.00,
        "target_reduction": -3.50,
        "actual_reduction": -3.50,
        "material": "BOSTON XO",
        "color": "CLEAR",
        "check_result": "PASS",
        "is_ordered": true,
        "expected_arrival_date": "2026-11-20"
      }
    ],
    "followup_days": "1,7,30,90",
    "replace_months": 12,
    "lead_time_days": 20,
    "sales_order_no": null
  },
  "trace_id": "b4c5d6e7f8a9b0c1"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `fit_no` 查 `m10_ortho_k_fitting` 2) 关联查 `status <> 'CANCELLED'` 的 `m10_ortho_k_custom_param`（按 `eye_side` 排序）3) 组装扁平结构（供 M16-04、M10-06、M01-08 直接使用，不返回从表全量）4) `followup_days` 取 `orthok.followup.nodes` 系统参数；`replace_months` 取验发记录或产品参数组；`lead_time_days` 取定制参数 5) **只读**，不产生任何写入 6) 结果缓存 Redis 120 秒（`m10:orthok:fitting:{fit_no}`），定制参数变更时失效 7) SLA P95 ≤ 150ms。

**调用方：** M16-04（收银时展示定制参数）、M10-06（开单引用）、M01-08（适配性复核）。
**错误码：** `"1004001"`（验配单不存在）、`"1004040"`（内部调用参数缺失时）

#### API-45 患者当前在戴镜验配单

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/by-patient/{patient_id}/active
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "found": true,
    "fit_no": "OK202610050002",
    "status": "FOLLOWING",
    "delivered_at": "2026-10-31T11:00:00+08:00",
    "expected_replace_date": "2027-10-31",
    "next_followup_date": "2026-11-07",
    "overdue_days": 0,
    "replace_round": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "c5d6e7f8a9b0c1d2"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 查 `patient_id` 对应 `status IN ('DELIVERED','FOLLOWING','REPLACE_DUE')` 的记录（走 `idx_m10_ortho_k_fitting_active`）2) 无记录返回 `found=false` 3) 多条时取 `delivered_at DESC` 第一条（正常业务下一人仅一套在戴镜）4) 只读，SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

**调用方：** M07 医生工作站（开立禁忌检查时提示）、M10-09（复查排程校验）。
**错误码：** `40001`（patient_id 非法）

#### API-46 取定制参数（供 M10-06 开单）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/custom-params/{fit_no}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fit_no": "OK202610310005",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "items": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "bc": 7.95,
        "rc": 7.25,
        "ac": 8.25,
        "pc": 9.20,
        "diameter": 11.00,
        "actual_reduction": -3.50,
        "material": "BOSTON XO",
        "color": "CLEAR",
        "manufacturer_code": "MDW",
        "price_ref": 3800.0000,
        "check_result": "PASS_WITH_WARN"
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "d6e7f8a9b0c1d2e3"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `fit_no` 查有效定制参数 2) 仅返回开单必需字段 3) `price_ref` 为快照值，**M10-06/M16-04 必须重新调 M01-08 `pricing` 取实时价**，本字段仅作参考 4) 只读，SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

**调用方：** M10-06（生成订单明细）。
**错误码：** `"1004001"`

#### API-47 复查到期清单（供 M10-09）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/followups/due?store_id=101&due_date=2026-11-07
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "fit_no": "OK202610050002",
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "张××",
        "guardian_phone": "13900005678",
        "next_followup_date": "2026-11-07",
        "next_followup_node": "D7",
        "overdue_days": 0,
        "followup_plan_id": 33012
      }
    ],
    "total": 12
  },
  "trace_id": "e7f8a9b0c1d2e3f4"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按 `next_followup_date = due_date`（缺省当前日期）与门店过滤 2) 返回 `followup_plan_id` 供 M10-09 关联 3) 只读，SLA P95 ≤ 200ms。

**调用方：** M10-09（复查提醒与召回）。
**错误码：** `40001`

#### API-48 M16-04 销售单回写

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/ortho-k/fitting/on-sales-order
Content-Type: application/json

{
  "fit_no": "OK202610310005",
  "sales_order_no": "XS202610310021",
  "sales_order_status": "PAID",
  "order_amount": 7600.00,
  "paid_amount": 7600.00,
  "m10_order_no": "PO202610310021"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "fit_no": "OK202610310005",
    "sales_order_no": "XS202610310021",
    "sales_order_status": "PAID",
    "synced": true
  },
  "trace_id": "f8a9b0c1d2e3f4a5"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等：按 `sales_order_no` 查已存在则只更新快照 2) 按 `fit_no` 定位验配单，不存在 `"1004001"` 3) **只更新快照字段** `sales_order_no`、`sales_order_status`、`order_amount`、`paid_amount`、`m10_order_no`，**不改写任何业务状态** 4) `paid_amount > order_amount + 0.01` 时拒绝 `40001`（防 M16 回传错误） 5) 写日志（`action` 取 `CREATE_FITTING` 附 `changed_fields`）6) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

> **硬规则：** 本接口只能写快照字段，绝不修改 `status`。M10 的业务状态由本模块状态机唯一维护。

**错误码：** `"1004001"`、`40001`

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/ortho_k.py
"""M10-04 OK镜与RGP验配随访 —— 路由层"""

from typing import Literal, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Query, status
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.api.deps import get_current_user, get_db, require_perm
from app.core.response import ApiResponse, PageResult
from app.schemas.m10.ortho_k import (
    ApplyAssessIn,
    ApplyDetailOut,
    CorneaOut,
    CorneaSaveIn,
    CustomParamIn,
    CustomParamOut,
    DeliveryAcceptIn,
    DeliveryIssueIn,
    FittingBriefOut,
    FittingCreateIn,
    FittingDetailOut,
    FittingListOut,
    FittingQueryIn,
    FollowupDueListOut,
    FollowupOut,
    FollowupSaveIn,
    ReplaceStartIn,
    StopWearIn,
    TrialEvalIn,
    TrialOccupyIn,
    TrialUseOut,
)
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_service import OrthoKService
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_followup_service import OrthoKFollowupService
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_trial_service import OrthoKTrialService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/ortho-k", tags=["M10-04 OK镜验配随访"])


def get_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> OrthoKService:
    """构造验配单服务。"""
    return OrthoKService(db)


def get_trial_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> OrthoKTrialService:
    """构造试戴片服务。"""
    return OrthoTrialService(db)


def get_followup_service(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)) -> OrthoKFollowupService:
    """构造随访服务。"""
    return OrthoKFollowupService(db)


# ---------- 验配单主档 ----------

@router.get(
    "/fittings",
    response_model=ApiResponse[FittingListOut],
    summary="验配单分页列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:view"))],
)
async def list_fittings(
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64, description="姓名/单号/手机号"),
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="验配单状态"),
    product_kind: Optional[Literal["ORTHO_K", "RGP"]] = Query(None),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    fitter_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    patient_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    start_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="申请日期起 yyyy-MM-dd"),
    end_date: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="申请日期止 yyyy-MM-dd"),
    followup_due_only: bool = Query(False, description="仅看复查到期"),
    overdue_only: bool = Query(False, description="仅看逾期"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[FittingListOut]:
    """分页查询验配单，支持多种筛选与数据权限过滤。"""
    q = FittingQueryIn(
        keyword=keyword,
        status=status,
        product_kind=product_kind,
        store_id=store_id,
        fitter_id=fitter_id,
        patient_id=patient_id,
        start_date=start_date,
        end_date=end_date,
        followup_due_only=followup_due_only,
        overdue_only=overdue_only,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size,
    )
    result = await svc.list_fittings(q)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.get(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[FittingDetailOut],
    summary="验配单详情",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:view"))],
)
async def get_fitting(
    fit_no: str,
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[FittingDetailOut]:
    """按验配单号取完整详情（含准入、角膜、试戴、定制、验发、随访）。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_fitting_detail(fit_no))


@router.post(
    "/fittings",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    summary="新建验配单",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def create_fitting(
    body: FittingCreateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """新建 OK 镜验配单。支持 Idempotency-Key 幂等。"""
    result = await svc.create_fitting(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/applies",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="提交准入评估",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def submit_apply(
    body: ApplyAssessIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """录入六大类 12 项准入指标并自动判定准入结论。"""
    result = await svc.submit_apply(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 角膜地形图参数 ----------

@router.get(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}/cornea",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="角膜参数列表",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:view"))],
)
async def list_cornea(
    fit_no: str,
    eye_side: Optional[Literal["OD", "OS"]] = Query(None),
    measure_stage: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """按验配单查角膜地形图参数记录（可按眼别与阶段筛选）。"""
    items = await svc.list_cornea(fit_no, eye_side=eye_side, measure_stage=measure_stage)
    return ApiResponse.ok({"items": items})


@router.post(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}/cornea",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="保存角膜参数",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def save_cornea(
    fit_no: str,
    body: CorneaSaveIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """保存角膜地形图参数，服务端重算派生值并返回推荐 BC/直径。"""
    result = await svc.save_cornea(fit_no, body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 试戴片占用（核心：互斥） ----------

@router.post(
    "/trial-uses/occupy",
    response_model=ApiResponse[TrialUseOut],
    summary="占用试戴片",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def occupy_trial_lens(
    body: TrialOccupyIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKTrialService = Depends(get_trial_service),
) -> ApiResponse[TrialUseOut]:
    """占用一片试戴片。由数据库部分唯一索引保证一片同一时刻只给一个患者。"""
    result = await svc.occupy(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/trial-uses/{use_id}/release",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="释放试戴片",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def release_trial_lens(
    use_id: int,
    body: dict,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKTrialService = Depends(get_trial_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """释放试戴片占用，可同时登记消毒或损伤。"""
    result = await svc.release(use_id, body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/trial-uses/{use_id}/evaluation",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="保存试戴评估与订片决策",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:update"))],
)
async def save_trial_eval(
    use_id: int,
    body: TrialEvalIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKTrialService = Depends(get_trial_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """保存荧光配适、定位、活动度、TFT 与订片决策，并推进验配单状态。"""
    result = await svc.save_evaluation(use_id, body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 定制参数与 fit-check ----------

@router.post(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}/custom-params",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="生成定制参数",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def create_custom_param(
    fit_no: str,
    body: CustomParamIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """生成定制参数并锁定所引用的角膜地形图记录。"""
    result = await svc.create_custom_param(fit_no, body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/custom-params/{param_id}/fit-check",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="调 M01-08 适配校验",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:update"))],
)
async def run_fit_check(
    param_id: int,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """调用 M01-08 ortho-k/fit-check 并把完整回执快照落库。"""
    result = await svc.run_fit_check(param_id, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/custom-params/{param_id}/order",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="确认下单下发厂家",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:audit"))],
)
async def order_custom_param(
    param_id: int,
    body: dict,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """fit-check 通过且注册证有效时下发厂家。"""
    result = await svc.order_custom_param(param_id, body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 到片验收与发放 ----------

@router.post(
    "/deliveries/accept",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="逐片验收",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:update"))],
)
async def accept_delivery(
    body: DeliveryAcceptIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """逐项核对定制单与实物参数、外观检查，给出验收结论。"""
    result = await svc.accept_delivery(body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/deliveries/issue",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="发放镜片",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:sign"))],
)
async def issue_delivery(
    body: DeliveryIssueIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """校验消毒、教学 ≥30 分钟与签字后发放，并生成复查计划。"""
    result = await svc.issue_delivery(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 随访 ----------

@router.get(
    "/followups/due",
    response_model=ApiResponse[FollowupDueListOut],
    summary="随访到期清单",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:view"))],
)
async def list_followup_due(
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    due_within_days: int = Query(0, ge=0, le=90),
    overdue_only: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    svc: OrthoKFollowupService = Depends(get_followup_service),
) -> ApiResponse[FollowupDueListOut]:
    """查询 1/7/30/90 天 + 每 3 月的随访到期与逾期患者。"""
    result = await svc.list_due(
        store_id=store_id,
        due_within_days=due_within_days,
        overdue_only=overdue_only,
        page=page,
        page_size=page_size,
    )
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/followups",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="保存随访记录",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:create"))],
)
async def save_followup(
    body: FollowupSaveIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKFollowupService = Depends(get_followup_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """保存戴镜随访临床结果，推进验配单状态并同步 M10-09 计划。"""
    result = await svc.save_followup(body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 停戴与换片 ----------

@router.post(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}/stop",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="登记停戴",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:update"))],
)
async def stop_wear(
    fit_no: str,
    body: StopWearIn,
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """登记停戴原因并取消未执行的复查计划。"""
    result = await svc.stop_wear(fit_no, body, operator=user)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


@router.post(
    "/fittings/{fit_no}/replace",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="启动换片",
    dependencies=[Depends(require_perm("m10:orthok:update"))],
)
async def start_replace(
    fit_no: str,
    body: ReplaceStartIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(get_current_user),
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """启动换片：原单置 REPLACED，新单 replace_round+1 并串链。"""
    result = await svc.start_replace(fit_no, body, operator=user, idem_key=idempotency_key)
    return ApiResponse.ok(result)


# ---------- 内部契约（M16-04 / M10-06 / M01-08 调用） ----------

internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/ortho-k", tags=["M10-04 内部契约"])


@internal_router.get(
    "/fitting/{fit_no}",
    response_model=ApiResponse[dict],
    summary="P-09 取 OK 镜验配单与定制参数",
)
async def internal_get_fitting(
    fit_no: str,
    svc: OrthoKService = Depends(get_service),
) -> ApiResponse[dict]:
    """P-09 契约：供 M16-04、M10-06、M01-08 取验配单与定制参数。"""
    return ApiResponse.ok(await svc.get_fitting_for_internal(fit_no))
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/ortho_k_trial_service.py
"""M10-04 试戴片占用与试戴评估服务（核心并发控制）"""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

import asyncpg
from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.models.m10.ortho_k import (
    M10OrthoKFitting,
    M10OrthoKTrialLens,
    M10OrthoKTrialUse,
)
from app.schemas.m10.ortho_k import TrialEvalIn, TrialOccupyIn
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_log_service import OrthoKLogService

# 试戴片互斥索引名（与数据结构文档 4.6 节一字不差）
ACTIVE_INDEX = "uk_m10_ortho_k_trial_use_active"
OCCUPY_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 240
DISINFECT_INTERVAL_DAYS = 7


class OrthoKTrialService:
    """试戴片台账、占用、释放与试戴评估服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = OrthoKLogService(db)

    # ---------- 占用（核心：数据库级互斥） ----------

    async def occupy(
        self,
        req: TrialOccupyIn,
        operator: Any,
        idem_key: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """占用一片试戴片。

        互斥保证完全依赖部分唯一索引 uk_m10_ortho_k_trial_use_active：
        未释放（released_at IS NULL）的记录中，同一 trial_lens_id 只允许一条。
        这样即使两个请求并发进入，第二个 INSERT 必然唯一冲突，不存在 TOCTOU 竞态。
        """
        # 1. 幂等
        if idem_key:
            hit = await redis_client.get(f"m10:idem:orthok:occupy:{idem_key}")
            if hit:
                return self._load_json(hit)

        # 2. 验配单状态与准入有效期
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(req.fitting_id)
        if fitting.status not in ("ASSESSED", "TRIAL"):
            raise BizException("1004004", f"验配单当前状态为 {fitting.status}，不可占用试戴片")
        if fitting.assessed_at is not None:
            age_days = (datetime.now() - fitting.assessed_at).days
            if age_days > 90:
                raise BizException("1004017", "准入评估已超 90 天有效期，请重新评估")

        # 3. 试戴片可用性
        lens = await self._get_trial_lens(req.trial_lens_id, req.trial_lens_no)
        if lens is None:
            raise BizException("1004020", "试戴片不存在")
        self._assert_lens_usable(lens)

        # 4. 记录参数快照
        snapshot = {
            "trial_bc": lens.bc,
            "trial_rc": lens.rc,
            "trial_ac": lens.ac,
            "trial_pc": lens.pc,
            "trial_diameter": lens.diameter,
            "trial_reduction": lens.reduction_power,
        }

        # 5. INSERT —— 唯一互斥点
        use_no = await self._next_no("OKT")
        use = M10OrthoKTrialUse(
            use_no=use_no,
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            trial_lens_id=lens.id,
            trial_lens_no=lens.trial_lens_no,
            patient_id=fitting.patient_id,
            eye_side=req.eye_side,
            trial_seq=req.trial_seq,
            occupied_at=datetime.now(),
            occupied_by=operator.id,
            occupied_by_name=operator.name,
            released_at=None,
            **snapshot,
        )
        self.db.add(use)
        try:
            await self.db.flush()
        except asyncpg.exceptions.UniqueViolationError as exc:
            await self.db.rollback()
            if ACTIVE_INDEX in str(exc):
                holder = await self._current_holder(lens.id)
                msg = (
                    f"该试戴片已被他人占用（{holder['name']} 于 {holder['at']} 占用），请重新选择"
                    if holder else "该试戴片已被他人占用，请重新选择"
                )
                raise BizException("1004019", msg) from exc
            raise

        # 6. 台账与主表联动
        lens.status = "IN_USE"
        if fitting.status == "ASSESSED":
            fitting.status = "TRIAL"
            fitting.trial_started_at = datetime.now()

        await self.db.flush()

        # 7. 日志
        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            entity_type="TRIAL_USE",
            entity_id=use.id,
            action="OCCUPY_TRIAL",
            action_desc=f"占用试戴片 {lens.trial_lens_no}",
            to_status="TRIAL",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        result = self._to_out(use)
        if idem_key:
            await redis_client.setex(f"m10:idem:orthok:occupy:{idem_key}", 86400,
                                     self._to_json(result))
        return result

    # ---------- 释放 ----------

    async def release(self, use_id: int, payload: Dict[str, Any],
                      operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """释放试戴片占用。释放后该行不再被互斥索引覆盖。"""
        use = await self._get_trial_use(use_id)
        if use.released_at is not None:
            raise BizException("1004004", "该试戴记录已释放")

        release_kind = payload.get("release_kind", "RETURNED")
        if release_kind not in ("RETURNED", "KEPT", "ABNORMAL"):
            raise BizException("40001", "释放类型不合法")

        now = datetime.now()
        use.released_at = now
        use.release_kind = release_kind
        use.released_by = operator.id
        use.occupied_minutes = int((now - use.occupied_at).total_seconds() // 60)

        lens = await self._get_trial_lens(use.trial_lens_id, None)
        lens.usage_count += 1

        if payload.get("damage_found"):
            lens.status = "DAMAGED"
            lens.damage_note = payload.get("damage_note") or "归还时发现损伤"
        elif payload.get("disinfected_after"):
            lens.status = "IN_STOCK"
            lens.last_disinfected_at = payload.get("disinfected_at") or now
            lens.disinfect_count += 1
            use.disinfected_after = True
            use.disinfected_at = lens.last_disinfected_at
        else:
            lens.status = "DISINFECTING"

        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=use.fitting_id,
            fit_no=use.fit_no,
            entity_type="TRIAL_USE",
            entity_id=use.id,
            action="RELEASE_TRIAL",
            action_desc=f"释放试戴片 {use.trial_lens_no}，类型 {release_kind}",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "id": use.id,
            "released_at": use.released_at.isoformat(),
            "release_kind": use.release_kind,
            "occupied_minutes": use.occupied_minutes,
            "trial_lens_status": lens.status,
        }

    # ---------- 试戴评估与订片决策 ----------

    async def save_evaluation(self, use_id: int, req: TrialEvalIn,
                              operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """保存试戴评估并推进验配单状态。"""
        use = await self._get_trial_use(use_id)

        # TFT 完整性
        if req.tft_performed and (req.tft_pattern is None or req.tft_report_id is None):
            raise BizException("40001", "已做 TFT 必须填写图形结论与报告 ID")

        # 决策规则
        if req.fit_quality == "UNACCEPTABLE" and req.decision in ("ORDER_AS_IS", "ADJUST"):
            raise BizException(
                "1004023", "配适判定为不可接受，不可直接按试戴片参数下单")
        if req.decision in ("RETRY", "ABORT"):
            if not req.decision_note or len(req.decision_note.strip()) < 10:
                raise BizException("1004023", "换片再试或放弃验配必须写明理由（≥10 字）")
        if req.decision == "ADJUST":
            if None in (req.suggested_bc, req.suggested_reduction, req.suggested_diameter):
                raise BizException("1004023", "调整后下单必须填写建议 BC/降度/直径")

        # 写回评估字段
        for field in (
            "fluo_central", "fluo_reverse", "fluo_align", "fluo_peripheral",
            "fit_quality", "lens_position", "decentered_direction", "lens_movement",
            "coverage_ratio", "va_trial", "over_refraction_sph", "tft_performed",
            "tft_pattern", "tft_ring_diameter", "tft_ring_integrity", "tft_report_id",
            "decision", "decision_note", "suggested_bc", "suggested_reduction",
            "suggested_diameter",
        ):
            value = getattr(req, field, None)
            if value is not None:
                setattr(use, field, value)

        # 验配单状态推进
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(req.fitting_id)
        if req.decision in ("ORDER_AS_IS", "ADJUST"):
            fitting.status = "CUSTOMIZING"
        elif req.decision == "ABORT":
            fitting.status = "STOPPED"
            fitting.stop_reason_code = "OTHER"
            fitting.stop_reason = req.decision_note
            fitting.stopped_at = datetime.now()

        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            entity_type="TRIAL_USE",
            entity_id=use.id,
            action="SAVE_TRIAL_EVAL",
            action_desc=f"试戴评估：配适 {req.fit_quality}，决策 {req.decision}",
            from_status="TRIAL",
            to_status=fitting.status,
            snapshot=self._to_json(self._to_out(use)),
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "id": use.id,
            "fit_quality": use.fit_quality,
            "decision": use.decision,
            "fitting_status": fitting.status,
            "suggested_bc": self._num(use.suggested_bc),
            "suggested_reduction": self._num(use.suggested_reduction),
            "suggested_diameter": self._num(use.suggested_diameter),
        }

    # ---------- 试戴片可用性校验 ----------

    def _assert_lens_usable(self, lens: M10OrthoKTrialLens) -> None:
        """校验试戴片可占用：状态、有效期、消毒间隔。"""
        if lens.status != "IN_STOCK":
            raise BizException(
                "1004020", f"试戴片状态为 {lens.status}，不可使用")
        today = datetime.now().date()
        if lens.expire_date is not None and lens.expire_date < today:
            raise BizException(
                "1004020", f"试戴片已于 {lens.expire_date} 过期，不可使用")
        if lens.last_disinfected_at is not None:
            idle_days = (datetime.now() - lens.last_disinfected_at).days
            if idle_days > DISINFECT_INTERVAL_DAYS:
                raise BizException(
                    "1004022", f"该片已 {idle_days} 天未消毒，请先消毒")
        if lens.disinfect_count > 200:
            raise BizException(
                "1004020", "该试戴片累计消毒超过 200 次，建议报废")

    # ---------- BC / 直径推荐（§3.4.1 / §3.4.2） ----------

    @staticmethod
    def recommend_bc(k1: Decimal, delta_mm: Decimal = Decimal("0.05")) -> Decimal:
        """按平坦 K 值推荐试戴片基弧 BC。

        BC = 337.5 / Kf(D) + Δ，结果按 0.05mm 对齐（ROUND_HALF_UP）。
        """
        kf_mm = Decimal("337.5") / k1
        raw = kf_mm + delta_mm
        stepped = (raw / Decimal("0.05")).quantize(
            Decimal("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * Decimal("0.05")
        if stepped < Decimal("7.00") or stepped > Decimal("9.50"):
            raise BizException(
                "1004028",
                f"推荐基弧 {stepped}mm 超出可定制范围 7.00~9.50mm")
        return stepped.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

    @staticmethod
    def recommend_diameter(hvid: Decimal, ratio: Decimal = Decimal("0.94")) -> Decimal:
        """按 HVID 推荐镜片直径，按 0.10mm 对齐并限幅 10.00~11.00mm。"""
        raw = hvid * ratio
        stepped = (raw / Decimal("0.10")).quantize(
            Decimal("1"), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP) * Decimal("0.10")
        stepped = max(Decimal("10.00"), min(Decimal("11.00"), stepped))
        return stepped.quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

    @staticmethod
    def k_window(bc: Decimal, tolerance_d: Decimal = Decimal("0.75")) -> tuple:
        """由试戴片 BC 反推可适配的角膜 K 值区间。"""
        k_flat = (Decimal("337.5") / bc).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
        return (k_flat - tolerance_d, k_flat + tolerance_d)

    # ---------- 内部工具 ----------

    async def _get_fitting(self, fitting_id: int) -> M10OrthoKFitting:
        fitting = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKFitting).where(
                M10OrthoKFitting.id == fitting_id,
                M10OrthoKFitting.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if fitting is None:
            raise BizException("1004001", "验配单不存在或已作废")
        return fitting

    async def _get_trial_lens(self, lens_id: Optional[int],
                              lens_no: Optional[str]) -> Optional[M10OrthoKTrialLens]:
        cond = (M10OrthoKTrialLens.id == lens_id) if lens_id else (
            M10OrthoKTrialLens.trial_lens_no == lens_no)
        return (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKTrialLens).where(
                cond, M10OrthoKTrialLens.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()

    async def _get_trial_use(self, use_id: int) -> M10OrthoKTrialUse:
        use = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKTrialUse).where(M10OrthoKTrialUse.id == use_id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if use is None:
            raise BizException("40400", "试戴记录不存在")
        return use

    async def _current_holder(self, lens_id: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """查当前占用者，用于友好提示（不作为互斥依据）。"""
        row = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKTrialUse.occupied_by_name, M10OrthoKTrialUse.occupied_at)
            .where(
                M10OrthoKTrialUse.trial_lens_id == lens_id,
                M10OrthoKTrialUse.released_at.is_(None),
                M10OrthoKTrialUse.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .limit(1)
        )).first()
        if row is None:
            return None
        return {"name": row[0], "at": row[1].strftime("%H:%M")}

    async def _next_no(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Redis 计数器生成单号。"""
        day = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        seq = await redis_client.incr(f"m10:code:{prefix}:{day}")
        return f"{prefix}{day}{seq:04d}"
```

```python
# app/services/m10/ortho_k_service.py
"""M10-04 验配单主流程服务：准入判定、定制参数、fit-check、发放"""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m10.m01_optical_client import M01OpticalClient
from app.clients.m10.m03_patient_client import M03PatientClient
from app.clients.m10.m10_followup_client import M10FollowupClient
from app.core.exceptions import BizException
from app.models.m10.ortho_k import (
    M10OrthoKApply,
    M10OrthoKCornea,
    M10OrthoKCustomParam,
    M10OrthoKDelivery,
    M10OrthoKFitting,
)
from app.schemas.m10.ortho_k import (
    ApplyAssessIn,
    CorneaSaveIn,
    CustomParamIn,
    DeliveryAcceptIn,
    DeliveryIssueIn,
    FittingCreateIn,
    ReplaceStartIn,
    StopWearIn,
)
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_log_service import OrthoKLogService
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_trial_service import OrthoKTrialService

# 准入阈值（对应附录 B 系统参数）
AGE_MIN, AGE_MAX = 8, 40
SPHERE_MIN, SPHERE_MAX = Decimal("-6.00"), Decimal("-0.75")
CYLINDER_LIMIT = Decimal("-1.50")
ANISOMETROPIA_LIMIT = Decimal("1.50")
K_MIN, K_MAX = Decimal("41.00"), Decimal("46.00")
THICKNESS_MIN = 480
IOP_MIN, IOP_MAX = Decimal("10"), Decimal("21")
BUT_MIN, SCHIRMER_MIN = Decimal("10"), Decimal("10")
FOLLOWUP_DAYS = "1,7,30,90"
NODE_DAYS = {"D1": 1, "D7": 7, "D30": 30, "D90": 90,
             "M3": 180, "M6": 270, "M9": 360, "M12": 450}


class OrthoKService:
    """OK 镜验配主流程服务。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.log = OrthoKLogService(db)
        self.trial = OrthoKTrialService(db)
        self.m01 = M01OpticalClient()
        self.m03 = M03PatientClient()
        self.m09 = M10FollowupClient()

    # ---------- 准入评估判定（§3.3.1 十二项） ----------

    @staticmethod
    def evaluate_access(req: ApplyAssessIn,
                        cylinder_limit: Decimal = CYLINDER_LIMIT) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """逐项判定 12 项准入指标并汇总结论，返回各 *_check_passed 与 access_result。"""
        od_km = (req.od_k1 + req.od_k2) / 2
        os_km = (req.os_k1 + req.os_k2) / 2
        aniso = abs(req.od_sphere - req.os_sphere)

        relaxed = {
            "AGE": not (AGE_MIN <= req.age <= AGE_MAX),
            "THICKNESS": min(req.od_cornea_thickness, req.os_cornea_thickness) < THICKNESS_MIN,
            "TEAR": (req.tear_break_up_time < BUT_MIN or req.schirmer_test < SCHIRMER_MIN),
            "ANISO": aniso > ANISOMETROPIA_LIMIT,
        }
        hard = {
            "SPHERE": not (SPHERE_MIN <= req.od_sphere <= SPHERE_MAX
                           and SPHERE_MIN <= req.os_sphere <= SPHERE_MAX),
            "CYLINDER": (req.od_cylinder < cylinder_limit
                         or req.os_cylinder < cylinder_limit),
            "CURVATURE": not (K_MIN <= od_km <= K_MAX and K_MIN <= os_km <= K_MAX),
            "IOP": not (IOP_MIN <= req.od_iop <= IOP_MAX
                        and IOP_MIN <= req.os_iop <= IOP_MAX),
            "TOPO": (req.topography_pattern in ("KERATOCONUS_SUSPECT", "IRREGULAR")
                     or req.keratoconus_suspect),
            "EYELID": (req.eyelid_condition == "LAGOPHTHALMOS"
                       or req.conjunctiva_condition == "SEVERE_HYPEREMIA"
                       or req.meibomian_gland == "SEVERE_DYSFUNCTION"),
            "COMPLIANCE": (req.compliance_score is not None and req.compliance_score < 60),
            "CONSENT": not (req.parent_consent_signed and req.consent_file_id is not None),
        }

        fail_items = [k for k, v in hard.items() if v]
        fail_items += [k for k, v in relaxed.items() if v]

        if not fail_items:
            result = "PASS"
        elif len(fail_items) <= 2 and all(k in relaxed for k in fail_items) \
                and all(relaxed[k] for k in fail_items):
            result = "CONDITIONAL"
        else:
            result = "FAIL"

        review_required = result == "CONDITIONAL" or \
            req.topography_pattern == "ASYMMETRIC_BOWTIE"

        return {
            "age_check_passed": not relaxed["AGE"],
            "sphere_check_passed": not hard["SPHERE"],
            "cylinder_check_passed": not hard["CYLINDER"],
            "curvature_check_passed": not hard["CURVATURE"],
            "thickness_check_passed": not relaxed["THICKNESS"],
            "iop_check_passed": not hard["IOP"],
            "tear_check_passed": not relaxed["TEAR"],
            "topography_check_passed": not hard["TOPO"],
            "eyelid_check_passed": not hard["EYELID"],
            "anisometropia": aniso,
            "anisometropia_check_passed": not relaxed["ANISO"],
            "access_result": result,
            "fail_items": fail_items,
            "doctor_review_required": review_required,
        }

    # ---------- 角膜参数保存与推荐（§3.4.1 / §3.4.2） ----------

    async def save_cornea(self, fit_no: str, req: CorneaSaveIn,
                          operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """保存角膜参数，服务端重算派生值并返回推荐 BC/直径。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no)
        if fitting.status not in ("APPLYING", "ASSESSED", "TRIAL", "CUSTOMIZING", "FOLLOWING"):
            raise BizException("1004004", f"验配单当前状态为 {fitting.status}，不可录入角膜参数")

        if req.k1 > req.k2:
            raise BizException("40001", "K1 必须小于等于 K2（K1 为平坦曲率）")
        if (req.thickness_central is not None and req.thickness_thinnest is not None
                and req.thickness_thinnest > req.thickness_central):
            raise BizException("40001", "最薄点角膜厚度不得大于中央角膜厚度")
        if (req.pupil_light is not None and req.pupil_dark is not None
                and req.pupil_dark < req.pupil_light):
            raise BizException("40001", "暗环境瞳孔直径不得小于明环境瞳孔直径")

        need_report = (req.topography_pattern in ("KERATOCONUS_SUSPECT", "IRREGULAR")
                       or req.keratoconus_suspect)
        if need_report and req.topography_report_id is None:
            raise BizException("1004018", "圆锥可疑记录必须上传地形图报告")

        km = ((req.k1 + req.k2) / 2).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
        astig = (req.k2 - req.k1).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))

        cornea = M10OrthoKCornea(
            cornea_no=await self._next_no("OCT"),
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            patient_id=fitting.patient_id,
            eye_side=req.eye_side,
            measure_stage=req.measure_stage,
            measure_source=req.measure_source,
            measured_at=datetime.now(),
            k1=req.k1,
            k2=req.k2,
            km=km,
            axis_k2=req.axis_k2,
            cornea_astigmatism=astig,
            k1_mm=(Decimal("337.5") / req.k1).quantize(Decimal("0.01")),
            k2_mm=(Decimal("337.5") / req.k2).quantize(Decimal("0.01")),
            e_value=req.e_value,
            hvid=req.hvid,
            pupil_light=req.pupil_light,
            pupil_dark=req.pupil_dark,
            thickness_central=req.thickness_central,
            thickness_thinnest=req.thickness_thinnest,
            topography_pattern=req.topography_pattern,
            keratoconus_suspect=req.keratoconus_suspect,
            topography_report_id=req.topography_report_id,
            topography_file_url=req.topography_file_url,
            device_code=req.device_code,
        )
        self.db.add(cornea)
        await self.db.flush()

        recommended_bc = None
        recommended_diameter = None
        try:
            recommended_bc = self.trial.recommend_bc(req.k1)
        except BizException:
            recommended_bc = None
        if req.hvid is not None:
            recommended_diameter = self.trial.recommend_diameter(req.hvid)

        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            entity_type="CORNEA",
            entity_id=cornea.id,
            action="SAVE_CORNEA",
            action_desc=f"保存{req.eye_side}角膜参数 {cornea.cornea_no}",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "id": cornea.id,
            "cornea_no": cornea.cornea_no,
            "km": float(km),
            "cornea_astigmatism": float(astig),
            "k1_mm": float(cornea.k1_mm),
            "k2_mm": float(cornea.k2_mm),
            "recommended_bc": float(recommended_bc) if recommended_bc else None,
            "recommended_diameter": float(recommended_diameter) if recommended_diameter else None,
        }

    # ---------- 定制参数生成 ----------

    async def create_custom_param(self, fit_no: str, req: CustomParamIn,
                                  operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """生成定制参数。校验曲率关系、追溯链，并锁定所引用的角膜记录。"""
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(fit_no)
        if fitting.status != "CUSTOMIZING":
            raise BizException("1004004", f"验配单当前状态为 {fitting.status}，不可生成定制参数")

        # 追溯链：角膜记录必须有效且为验配前测量
        cornea = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKCornea).where(M10OrthoKCornea.id == req.cornea_id)
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if cornea is None or not cornea.is_valid:
            raise BizException("1004018", "所引用的角膜参数记录不存在或已作废")
        if cornea.measure_stage != "PRE_FIT":
            raise BizException("1004018", "定制参数只能引用验配前地形图")

        # 弧参数关系：RC < BC < AC < PC
        if req.rc is not None and req.rc >= req.bc:
            raise BizException("1004028", "反转弧 RC 必须小于基弧 BC")
        if req.ac is not None and req.ac <= req.bc:
            raise BizException("1004028", "定位弧 AC 必须大于基弧 BC")
        if req.pc is not None and req.ac is not None and req.pc <= req.ac:
            raise BizException("1004028", "周边弧 PC 必须大于定位弧 AC")
        if req.diameter <= req.bc:
            raise BizException("1004028", "镜片直径必须大于基弧")

        diff = (req.actual_reduction - req.target_reduction).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
        if abs(diff) >= Decimal("0.001"):
            if not req.reduction_adjust_reason or \
                    len(req.reduction_adjust_reason.strip()) < 10:
                raise BizException("1004028", "降度与目标不一致时必须写明调整原因（≥10 字）")

        param = M10OrthoKCustomParam(
            custom_no=await self._next_no("OKC"),
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            patient_id=fitting.patient_id,
            eye_side=req.eye_side,
            trial_use_id=req.trial_use_id,
            cornea_id=req.cornea_id,
            param_source=req.param_source,
            bc=req.bc,
            rc=req.rc,
            ac=req.ac,
            pc=req.pc,
            diameter=req.diameter,
            target_reduction=req.target_reduction,
            actual_reduction=req.actual_reduction,
            reduction_diff=diff,
            reduction_adjust_reason=req.reduction_adjust_reason,
            over_refraction=req.over_refraction,
            material=req.material,
            color=req.color,
            manufacturer_code=req.manufacturer_code,
            manufacture_lead_days=req.manufacture_lead_days,
            check_result="PENDING",
        )
        self.db.add(param)
        cornea.is_locked = True
        await self.db.flush()

        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            entity_type="CUSTOM_PARAM",
            entity_id=param.id,
            action="SAVE_CUSTOM_PARAM",
            action_desc=f"生成{req.eye_side}定制参数 {param.custom_no}",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "id": param.id,
            "custom_no": param.custom_no,
            "status": param.status,
            "check_result": param.check_result,
            "reduction_diff": float(diff),
            "fitting_status": fitting.status,
        }

    # ---------- fit-check（M01-08 强制校验） ----------

    async def run_fit_check(self, param_id: int, operator: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """调用 M01-08 GET /internal/v1/optical/ortho-k/fit-check 并存完整回执。

        任何上游异常都不得降级为「通过」——宁可阻断业务，也不允许未校验下单。
        """
        param = await self._get_custom_param(param_id)
        fitting = await self._get_fitting(param.fit_no)
        cornea = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKCornea).where(M10OrthoKCornea.id == param.cornea_id)
        )).scalar_one()

        started = datetime.now()
        try:
            resp = await self.m01.ortho_k_fit_check(
                product_code=fitting.product_code,
                cornea_k=cornea.km,
                cornea_thickness=cornea.thickness_central,
                astigmatism=fitting.od_cylinder if param.eye_side == "OD"
                else fitting.os_cylinder,
                myopia=fitting.od_sphere if param.eye_side == "OD" else fitting.os_sphere,
                age=fitting.age,
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            raise BizException(
                "1004026",
                "适配校验服务暂时不可用，无法下单（不允许跳过校验）",
            ) from exc

        elapsed_ms = int((datetime.now() - started).total_seconds() * 1000)
        checks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = resp.get("checks", [])
        recommended: List[Dict[str, Any]] = resp.get("recommended_params", [])
        fit_ok = bool(resp.get("fit")) and all(c.get("passed") for c in checks)

        fail_items = [c.get("item") for c in checks if not c.get("passed")]

        # 与推荐参数比对，判断是否降级为 PASS_WITH_WARN
        deviations: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        if fit_ok and recommended:
            rec = recommended[0]
            pairs = [
                ("BC", param.bc, rec.get("base_curve"), Decimal("0.50")),
                ("降度", param.actual_reduction, rec.get("reduction_power"), Decimal("0.50")),
                ("直径", param.diameter, rec.get("diameter"), Decimal("0.50")),
            ]
            for name, actual, recommend, limit in pairs:
                if recommend is None or actual is None:
                    continue
                d = (Decimal(str(actual)) - Decimal(str(recommend))).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
                deviations.append({
                    "item": name,
                    "actual": float(actual),
                    "recommended": float(recommend),
                    "diff": float(d),
                    "warn": abs(d) > limit,
                })

        if not fit_ok:
            check_result = "FAIL"
        elif any(d["warn"] for d in deviations):
            check_result = "PASS_WITH_WARN"
        else:
            check_result = "PASS"

        param.fit_check_at = datetime.now()
        param.fit_check_passed = fit_ok
        param.fit_check_result = resp
        param.fit_check_message = resp.get("message")
        param.check_result = check_result
        param.check_fail_items = fail_items or None
        if check_result != "FAIL":
            param.status = "CHECKED"

        await self.log.record(
            fitting_id=fitting.id,
            fit_no=fitting.fit_no,
            entity_type="CUSTOM_PARAM",
            entity_id=param.id,
            action="FIT_CHECK",
            action_desc=f"适配校验：{check_result}",
            duration_ms=elapsed_ms,
            result="SUCCESS" if fit_ok else "FAIL",
            error_code=None if fit_ok else "1004026",
            error_message=None if fit_ok else f"校验不通过项：{fail_items}",
            operator=operator,
        )
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "id": param.id,
            "check_result": check_result,
            "fit_check_passed": fit_ok,
            "fit_check_at": param.fit_check_at.isoformat(),
            "fit_check_message": param.fit_check_message,
            "fit_check_result": resp,
            "deviations": deviations,
        }

    # ---------- 内部工具 ----------

    async def _get_fitting(self, fit_no: str) -> M10OrthoKFitting:
        fitting = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKFitting).where(
                M10OrthoKFitting.fit_no == fit_no,
                M10OrthoKFitting.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if fitting is None:
            raise BizException("1004001", "验配单不存在或已作废")
        return fitting

    async def _get_custom_param(self, param_id: int) -> M10OrthoKCustomParam:
        param = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M10OrthoKCustomParam).where(
                M10OrthoKCustomParam.id == param_id,
                M10OrthoKCustomParam.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalar_one_or_none()
        if param is None:
            raise BizException("40400", "定制参数不存在")
        return param

    async def _next_no(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        from app.core.redis import redis_client
        day = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        seq = await redis_client.incr(f"m10:code:{prefix}:{day}")
        return f"{prefix}{day}{seq:04d}"
```

**代码说明：**

| 类/方法 | 职责 | 关键点 |
|---|---|---|
| `OrthoKTrialService.occupy` | 试戴片占用 | **互斥完全交给 `uk_m10_ortho_k_trial_use_active`**；捕获 `UniqueViolationError` 转 `"1004019"` 并附当前占用者 |
| `OrthoKTrialService.release` | 释放与消毒 | 释放后该行脱离互斥索引覆盖；消毒或损伤联动台账状态 |
| `OrthoKTrialService.save_evaluation` | 试戴评估 | 配适 `UNACCEPTABLE` 阻断「直接下单」；`RETRY`/`ABORT` 强制理由 |
| `OrthoKTrialService.recommend_bc` | BC 推荐 | `BC = 337.5/Kf + Δ`，0.05mm 对齐，越界 `"1004028"` |
| `OrthoKTrialService.recommend_diameter` | 直径推荐 | `HVID × ratio`，0.10mm 对齐，限幅 10.00~11.00 |
| `OrthoKService.evaluate_access` | 准入判定 | 12 项分 `hard`/`relaxed` 两组；≤2 项 relaxed 不达标 → `CONDITIONAL` |
| `OrthoKService.save_cornea` | 角膜参数 | 派生值全部服务端重算，不采信前端 |
| `OrthoKService.create_custom_param` | 定制参数 | 追溯链校验 + 弧参数关系 + 锁定角膜记录 |
| `OrthoKService.run_fit_check` | 适配校验 | **上游异常一律 `"1004026"`，绝不降级为通过**；完整回执落 `fit_check_result` |

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m10:orthok:dashboard:{store_id}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 工作台首页 7 个待办计数 | 到期自然失效，或验配单状态变更时主动删 | 20 门店 × 3KB ≈ 60KB |
| `m10:orthok:fitting:{fit_no}` | String(JSON) | 120s | **P-09 内部契约**返回体缓存 | 定制参数变更、状态流转、验发时删 | 2,000 在册 × 5KB ≈ 10MB |
| `m10:orthok:fitting:patient:{patient_id}` | String(JSON) | 300s | 患者当前在戴镜验配单 | 发放、换片、停戴时删 | 3,000 患者 × 1KB ≈ 3MB |
| `m10:orthok:trial:stock:{store_id}` | Hash | 30s | 试戴片分状态计数（P01 库存概览） | 占用/释放/消毒/报废时 `HINCRBY` | 20 门店 × 10 字段 ≈ 2KB |
| `m10:orthok:trial:match:{fitting_id}:{eye}` | String(JSON) | 60s | 试戴片智能匹配结果 | 占用/释放时删该门店全部匹配键 | 50/日 × 8KB ≈ 400KB |
| `m10:orthok:code:{prefix}:{yyyyMMdd}` | String(INT) | 48h | 单号计数器（OKA/OK/OCT/OKT/OKC/OKD/OKF） | 每日 03:00 与 DB `max()` 对账 | 7 前缀 × 100B ≈ 1KB |
| `m10:idem:orthok:{biz}:{key}` | String(JSON) | 24h | 幂等键（create/occupy/order/issue/replace） | 到期自然失效 | 500/日 × 4KB ≈ 2MB |
| `m10:lock:orthok:fitting:{id}` | String | 60s | 验配单编辑锁（防两人同时改同一单） | 释放或超时 | 并发 10 × 100B ≈ 1KB |
| `m10:orthok:sync:followup_plan` | List | — | M10-09 复查计划同步补偿队列 | 同步成功后 `LPOP` | 峰值 200 条 × 1KB ≈ 200KB |
| `m10:orthok:overdue:queue` | ZSet | — | 逾期患者队列（score = overdue_days） | 每日重算 | 200 × 100B ≈ 20KB |
| `m10:orthok:trial:timeout` | ZSet | — | 试戴片超时占用告警队列（score = occupied_at 时间戳） | 释放时 `ZREM` | 50 × 100B ≈ 5KB |

**总容量估算：约 16MB**（Redis 7 单实例可轻松承载）。

**缓存一致性策略：**

| 缓存 | 一致性要求 | 策略 |
|---|---|---|
| `dashboard` | 允许 60 秒延迟 | Cache-Aside，60s TTL |
| `fitting`（P-09） | **强一致优先** | 写操作成功后立即 `DEL`；TTL 120s 兜底 |
| `trial:stock` | 允许 30 秒延迟 | Hash 增量更新（`HINCRBY`），避免全量重算 |
| `trial:match` | 允许 60 秒延迟 | 占用/释放时按门店 `SCAN` + `DEL`；TTL 兜底 |
| `fitting:patient` | **强一致优先** | 发放/换片/停戴后 `DEL` |

```python
# app/core/cache/m10_ortho_k_cache.py
"""M10-04 缓存封装：统一的 Key 构造与失效"""

from typing import Any, Optional

from app.core.redis import redis_client


class OrthoKCache:
    """OK 镜验配缓存管理。"""

    DASHBOARD_TTL = 60
    FITTING_TTL = 120
    PATIENT_TTL = 300
    TRIAL_STOCK_TTL = 30
    TRIAL_MATCH_TTL = 60

    @staticmethod
    def dashboard_key(store_id: int) -> str:
        return f"m10:orthok:dashboard:{store_id}"

    @staticmethod
    def fitting_key(fit_no: str) -> str:
        return f"m10:orthok:fitting:{fit_no}"

    @staticmethod
    def patient_key(patient_id: int) -> str:
        return f"m10:orthok:fitting:patient:{patient_id}"

    @staticmethod
    def trial_stock_key(store_id: int) -> str:
        return f"m10:orthok:trial:stock:{store_id}"

    @staticmethod
    def trial_match_pattern(fitting_id: int) -> str:
        return f"m10:orthok:trial:match:{fitting_id}:*"

    async def invalidate_fitting(self, fit_no: str, patient_id: int,
                                 store_id: int) -> None:
        """验配单相关缓存统一失效：P-09、患者在戴、工作台、匹配结果。"""
        async for key in redis_client.scan_iter(match=self.trial_match_pattern(0)[:-1] + "*"):
            await redis_client.delete(key)
        await redis_client.delete(
            self.fitting_key(fit_no),
            self.patient_key(patient_id),
            self.dashboard_key(store_id),
        )

    async def get_fitting(self, fit_no: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """读 P-09 缓存。"""
        return await redis_client.get(self.fitting_key(fit_no))

    async def set_fitting(self, fit_no: str, payload: Any) -> None:
        """写 P-09 缓存。"""
        await redis_client.setex(self.fitting_key(fit_no), self.FITTING_TTL, payload)

    async def incr_trial_stock(self, store_id: int, from_status: str,
                               to_status: str) -> None:
        """试戴片状态迁移时的增量计数（避免全量重算）。"""
        key = self.trial_stock_key(store_id)
        pipe = redis_client.pipeline()
        pipe.hincrby(key, from_status, -1)
        pipe.hincrby(key, to_status, 1)
        pipe.expire(key, self.TRIAL_STOCK_TTL)
        await pipe.execute()
```

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

| 任务名 | 触发时机 | 处理内容 | 重试策略 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `m10.orthok.trial.timeout_scan` | APScheduler 每 15 分钟 | 扫描 `released_at IS NULL AND occupied_at < now-4h` 的占用，写入告警队列并发站内提醒 | 不重试（幂等扫描） |
| `m10.orthok.trial.expire_scan` | APScheduler 每日 01:00 | 扫描 `expire_date < CURRENT_DATE` 的试戴片置 `EXPIRED`；`disinfect_count > 200` 提示报废 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.trial.disinfect_remind` | APScheduler 每日 08:00 | 距上次消毒 >7 天的在库试戴片，推送给试戴片管理员 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.arrival.followup` | APScheduler 每日 09:00 | 扫描 `status='ORDERED'` 且 `expected_arrival_date < CURRENT_DATE` 的超期未到片，提醒跟催 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.replace.warn` | APScheduler 每日 09:30 | 距 `expected_replace_date ≤60` 天的验配单转 `REPLACE_DUE` 并提醒 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.followup.overdue` | APScheduler 每日 08:30 | 重算 `overdue_days`，>30 天推送脱管专项给主管 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.followup.remind` | APScheduler 每日 09:00 | 对今日应复查患者调 M03-05 发提醒（7 天内最多 1 次） | 重试 3 次，指数退避 60/180/540s |
| `m10.orthok.followup.plan_sync` | 发放/随访后 | 调 M10-09 P-26 同步复查计划 | 重试 5 次，指数退避；失败入 `m10:orthok:sync:followup_plan` |
| `m10.orthok.report.alert` | 上传后 1 小时 | 检查 `report_required=TRUE AND report_id IS NULL`，存在则持续告警 | 不重试 |
| `m10.orthok.log.partition` | APScheduler 每月 20 日 | 预建下下月日志分区；DETACH 24 个月前的分区 | 重试 2 次 |
| `m10.orthok.export` | 用户触发 | 生成 Excel 导出任务 | 重试 2 次 |
| `m10.orthok.code.reconcile` | APScheduler 每日 03:00 | 单号计数器与数据库 `max()` 对账 | 不重试 |

```python
# app/tasks/m10/ortho_k_tasks.py
"""M10-04 Celery 异步任务"""

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from celery import shared_task
from celery.utils.log import get_task_logger
from sqlalchemy import and_, select, update

from app.core.redis import redis_client
from app.core.session import get_async_session
from app.models.m10.ortho_k import (
    M10OrthoKFitting,
    M10OrthoKTrialLens,
    M10OrthoKTrialUse,
)
from app.services.m10.ortho_k_log_service import OrthoKLogService

logger = get_task_logger(__name__)

OCCUPY_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 240
DISINFECT_INTERVAL_DAYS = 7
DISINFECT_MAX_TIMES = 200


@shared_task(name="m10.orthok.trial.timeout_scan")
def scan_trial_occupy_timeout() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """扫描超时未归还的试戴片占用（>4 小时）并告警。

    试戴片若长期挂在某患者名下，会导致其他患者无法使用该片，
    因此必须有专门的巡检任务兜底（前端提示不足以保证及时归还）。
    """
    threshold = datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=OCCUPY_TIMEOUT_MINUTES)
    alerted: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    async def _run() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            rows = (await db.execute(
                select(M10OrthoKTrialUse).where(
                    M10OrthoKTrialUse.released_at.is_(None),
                    M10OrthoKTrialUse.occupied_at < threshold,
                    M10OrthoKTrialUse.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalars().all()

            for use in rows:
                minutes = int((datetime.now() - use.occupied_at).total_seconds() // 60)
                await redis_client.zadd(
                    "m10:orthok:trial:timeout",
                    {f"{use.id}": use.occupied_at.timestamp()},
                )
                alerted.append({
                    "use_id": use.id,
                    "trial_lens_no": use.trial_lens_no,
                    "patient_id": use.patient_id,
                    "occupied_minutes": minutes,
                })

            if alerted:
                await OrthoKLogService(db).record_system(
                    action="FORCE_RELEASE_TRIAL",
                    action_desc=f"扫描到 {len(alerted)} 条超时未归还试戴片占用",
                    entity_type="TRIAL_USE",
                )
                await db.commit()
            return alerted

    import asyncio
    result = asyncio.run(_run())
    logger.info("试戴片超时扫描完成，告警 %d 条", len(result))
    return {"alerted": len(result), "items": result}


@shared_task(name="m10.orthok.followup.overdue", bind=True, max_retries=2)
def recalc_followup_overdue(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """重算逾期天数。逾期 >30 天进入脱管专项监控。

    overdue_days 是冗余字段（由本任务维护），用于加速到期扫描与告警统计；
    与 M10-09 的计划对账差异 >1 天时输出告警日志。
    """
    async def _run() -> int:
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            today = datetime.now().date()
            rows = (await db.execute(
                select(M10OrthoKFitting).where(
                    M10OrthoKFitting.status.in_(["DELIVERED", "FOLLOWING", "REPLACE_DUE"]),
                    M10OrthoKFitting.next_followup_date.isnot(None),
                    M10OrthoKFitting.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalars().all()

            changed = 0
            queue: Dict[str, float] = {}
            for f in rows:
                days = max(0, (today - f.next_followup_date).days)
                if days != f.overdue_days:
                    f.overdue_days = days
                    changed += 1
                if days > 0:
                    queue[str(f.id)] = float(days)

            await redis_client.delete("m10:orthok:overdue:queue")
            if queue:
                await redis_client.zadd("m10:orthok:overdue:queue", queue)

            await db.commit()
            return changed

    import asyncio
    try:
        changed = asyncio.run(_run())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        logger.warning("逾期重算失败，重试：%s", exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (2 ** self.request.retries))
    logger.info("逾期天数重算完成，更新 %d 条", changed)
    return {"updated": changed}


@shared_task(name="m10.orthok.replace.warn")
def scan_replace_due() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """扫描换片预警：距预计换片日 ≤60 天的验配单置 REPLACE_DUE。"""
    async def _run() -> int:
        async with get_async_session() as db:
            today = datetime.now().date()
            limit = today + timedelta(days=60)
            result = await db.execute(
                update(M10OrthoKFitting)
                .where(
                    M10OrthoKFitting.status == "FOLLOWING",
                    M10OrthoKFitting.expected_replace_date.isnot(None),
                    M10OrthoKFitting.expected_replace_date <= limit,
                    M10OrthoKFitting.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
                .values(status="REPLACE_DUE")
            )
            await db.commit()
            return result.rowcount or 0

    import asyncio
    count = asyncio.run(_run())
    logger.info("换片预警扫描完成，置 REPLACE_DUE %d 条", count)
    return {"updated": count}


@shared_task(name="m10.orthok.followup.plan_sync", bind=True, max_retries=5)
def sync_followup_plan(self, fitting_id: int, delivered_at: str,
                       replace_months: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """同步复查计划到 M10-09（P-26）。失败进补偿队列，指数退避重试。

    复查节点 1/7/30/90 天 + 每 3 月由本任务计算并传参，
    M10-09 负责落库与后续提醒；本模块只在本地冗余 next_followup_date 兜底。
    """
    from app.clients.m10.m10_followup_client import M10FollowupClient

    node_days = {"D1": 1, "D7": 7, "D30": 30, "D90": 90,
                 "M3": 180, "M6": 270, "M9": 360, "M12": 450}

    async def _run() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        start = datetime.fromisoformat(delivered_at).date()
        plans = [
            {
                "node": node,
                "due_date": (start + timedelta(days=days)).isoformat(),
                "followup_type": "POST_ORTHOK",
            }
            for node, days in node_days.items()
        ]
        client = M10FollowupClient()
        return await client.on_plan_created(
            fitting_id=fitting_id,
            plans=plans,
            replace_months=replace_months,
        )

    import asyncio
    try:
        result = asyncio.run(_run())
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        payload = f"{fitting_id}|{delivered_at}|{replace_months}"
        import json
        asyncio.run(redis_client.lpush("m10:orthok:sync:followup_plan", payload))
        logger.warning("复查计划同步失败，已入补偿队列：%s", exc)
        raise self.retry(exc=exc, countdown=60 * (3 ** self.request.retries))

    logger.info("复查计划同步成功 fitting_id=%s", fitting_id)
    return {"fitting_id": fitting_id, "plan_result": result}


@shared_task(name="m10.orthok.report.alert")
def alert_unreported_complications() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """扫描需上报但未上报的不良事件，持续告警直到闭环。"""
    async def _run() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        from app.models.m10.ortho_k import M10OrthoKFollowup

        async with get_async_session() as db:
            rows = (await db.execute(
                select(M10OrthoKFollowup).where(
                    M10OrthoKFollowup.report_required.is_(True),
                    M10OrthoKFollowup.report_id.is_(None),
                    M10OrthoKFollowup.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )).scalars().all()
            return [
                {
                    "followup_no": r.followup_no,
                    "fit_no": r.fit_no,
                    "patient_name": r.patient_name,
                    "visited_at": r.visited_at.isoformat() if r.visited_at else None,
                }
                for r in rows
            ]

    import asyncio
    items = asyncio.run(_run())
    if items:
        logger.error("存在 %d 条未上报不良事件，需立即处理", len(items))
    return {"pending": len(items), "items": items}
```

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务 ID | Cron 表达式 | 时区 | 说明 | 失败处理 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `orthok_trial_timeout_scan` | `*/15 * * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 试戴片超时占用扫描（>4 小时） | 记日志，下次继续 |
| `orthok_trial_expire_scan` | `0 1 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 试戴片到期置 `EXPIRED`、消毒超次提示报废 | 记日志 |
| `orthok_trial_disinfect_remind` | `0 8 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 消毒超期提醒（>7 天） | 记日志 |
| `orthok_followup_overdue` | `30 8 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 重算 `overdue_days` 与脱管队列 | 重试 2 次 |
| `orthok_followup_remind` | `0 9 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 今日应复查患者提醒（7 天内最多 1 次） | 重试 3 次 |
| `orthok_arrival_followup` | `0 9 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 超期未到片跟催 | 记日志 |
| `orthok_replace_warn` | `30 9 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 换片预警置 `REPLACE_DUE` | 记日志 |
| `orthok_code_reconcile` | `0 3 * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 单号计数器对账 | 记日志 |
| `orthok_log_partition` | `0 2 20 * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 日志分区预建与归档 | 重试 2 次 |
| `orthok_plan_compensate` | `*/10 * * * *` | Asia/Shanghai | 复查计划同步补偿队列消费 | 记日志 |

```python
# app/jobs/m10/ortho_k_jobs.py
"""M10-04 APScheduler 定时任务注册"""

from apscheduler.schedulers.asyncio import AsyncIOScheduler
from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger

from app.tasks.m10.ortho_k_tasks import (
    alert_unreported_complications,
    recalc_followup_overdue,
    scan_replace_due,
    scan_trial_occupy_timeout,
)

TIMEZONE = "Asia/Shanghai"


def register_orthok_jobs(scheduler: AsyncIOScheduler) -> None:
    """注册 M10-04 全部定时任务。"""
    scheduler.add_job(
        scan_trial_occupy_timeout,
        CronTrigger(minute="*/15", timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="orthok_trial_timeout_scan",
        name="试戴片超时占用扫描",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=300,
    )

    scheduler.add_job(
        recalc_followup_overdue,
        CronTrigger(hour=8, minute=30, timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="orthok_followup_overdue",
        name="随访逾期天数重算",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=1800,
    )

    scheduler.add_job(
        scan_replace_due,
        CronTrigger(hour=9, minute=30, timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="orthok_replace_warn",
        name="换片预警扫描",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=1800,
    )

    scheduler.add_job(
        alert_unreported_complications,
        CronTrigger(hour="9,15,21", minute=0, timezone=TIMEZONE),
        id="orthok_report_alert",
        name="未上报不良事件告警",
        replace_existing=True,
        max_instances=1,
        coalesce=True,
        misfire_grace_time=600,
    )
```
